\displaystyle J_{(c),10}^{(1)}(t;t_{0})=

\displaystyle\Pi_{b}(1)\Pi_{a}^{*}(1)\int d^{2}\xi_{0}d^{2}\bar{\xi}_{0}\mathcal{J}^{(1)}_{(c)}(\xi^{*}_{a},\bar{\xi}_{b},t;\xi_{0},\bar{\xi}^{*}_{0},t_{0})\langle\xi_{0}|0\rangle\langle 0|\bar{\xi}_{0}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{m}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{m}}dt_{k}\int d\bar{\xi}_{N+1}d\xi_{N+1}^{*}\prod_{n=0}^{N}d^{2}\xi_{n}d^{2}\bar{\xi}_{n}e^{-\xi_{n}^{*}\xi_{n}}e^{-\bar{\xi}_{n}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{n}}\prod_{n=1}^{N+1}e^{\xi_{n}^{*}\xi_{n-1}p_{n}}e^{\bar{\xi}_{n-1}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{n}p_{n}^{*}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bar{\xi}_{m}}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{*}(t_{m}-t_{k})]{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\xi^{*}_{k}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{m}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{m}}dt_{k}\;[-{\rm g}_{+}^{*}(t_{m}-t_{k})]\int{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}d\bar{\xi}_{N+1}d\xi_{N+1}^{*}}\prod_{n=0}^{k-1}d^{2}\xi_{n}e^{-\xi_{n}^{*}\xi_{n}}\prod_{n=0}^{m-1}d^{2}\bar{\xi}_{n}e^{-\bar{\xi}_{n}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{n}}

\displaystyle\times{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}d\xi_{k}^{*}}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\prod_{n=k+1}^{N}d\xi_{n-1}d\xi^{*}_{n}}\right){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}d\xi_{N}}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\prod_{n=k+1}^{N}\xi^{*}_{n}\xi_{n-1}p_{n}}\right){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\xi_{N+1}^{*}\xi_{N}p_{N+1}}

\displaystyle\times{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}d\bar{\xi}_{N}^{*}}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\prod_{n=N}^{m+1}d\bar{\xi}_{n}d\bar{\xi}^{*}_{n-1}}\right){\color[rgb]{1,0,0}d\bar{\xi}_{m}}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\prod_{n=m+1}^{N}\bar{\xi}^{*}_{n-1}\bar{\xi}_{n}p^{*}_{n}}\right){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bar{\xi}^{*}_{N}\bar{\xi}_{N+1}p_{N+1}^{*}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bar{\xi}_{m}\xi^{*}_{k}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{m}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{m}}dt_{k}\;[-{\rm g}_{+}^{*}(t_{m}-t_{k})]\int\prod_{n=0}^{k-1}d^{2}\xi_{n}e^{-\xi_{n}^{*}\xi_{n}}\prod_{n=0}^{m-1}d^{2}\bar{\xi}_{n}e^{-\bar{\xi}_{n}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{n}}

(290)

\displaystyle\times\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\prod_{n=k+1}^{N}d\xi_{n-1}d\xi^{*}_{n}}\right)\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\prod_{n=k+1}^{N}\xi^{*}_{n}\xi_{n-1}p_{n}}\right){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}p_{N+1}}

\displaystyle\times\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\prod_{n=N}^{m+1}d\bar{\xi}_{n}d\bar{\xi}^{*}_{n-1}}\right)\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\prod_{n=m+1}^{N}\bar{\xi}^{*}_{n-1}\bar{\xi}_{n}p^{*}_{n}}\right){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}p_{N+1}^{*}}\int{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}d\xi_{k}^{*}d\bar{\xi}_{m}\bar{\xi}_{m}\xi_{k}^{*}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{m}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{m}}dt_{k}\;[-{\rm g}_{+}^{*}(t_{m}-t_{k})]\prod_{n=k+1}^{N+1}p_{n}\prod_{n=m+1}^{N+1}p_{n}^{*}\int d\xi_{k}^{*}d\bar{\xi}_{m}\bar{\xi}_{m}\xi_{k}^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{m}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{m}}dt_{k}\;[-{\rm g}_{+}^{*}(t_{m}-t_{k})]\prod_{n=k+1}^{m}p_{n}

\displaystyle\longrightarrow

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{2}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{2}}dt_{1}\;[-{\rm g}_{+}^{*}(t_{2}-t_{1})]e^{-\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}\epsilon(t_{2}-t_{1})}

Analogously, for the path
(d)
in Fig.
30
we obtain

\displaystyle J_{(d)10}^{(1)}(t;,t_{0})=

\displaystyle\Pi_{b}(1)\Pi_{a}^{*}(1)\int d^{2}\xi_{0}d^{2}\bar{\xi}_{0}\mathcal{J}^{(1)}_{(\bar{\sigma})}(\xi^{*}_{a},\bar{\xi}_{b},t;\xi_{0},\bar{\xi}^{*}_{0},t_{0})\langle\xi_{0}|0\rangle\langle 0|\bar{\xi}_{0}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{m}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{m}}dt_{k}\int d\bar{\xi}_{N+1}d\xi_{N+1}^{*}\prod_{n=0}^{N}d^{2}\xi_{n}d^{2}\bar{\xi}_{n}e^{-\xi_{n}^{*}\xi_{n}}e^{-\bar{\xi}_{n}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{n}}\prod_{n=1}^{N+1}e^{\xi_{n}^{*}\xi_{n-1}p_{n}}e^{\bar{\xi}_{n-1}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{n}p_{n}^{*}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\xi_{m}^{*}}{\rm g}_{+}(t_{m}-t_{k}){\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bar{\xi}_{k}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{m}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{m}}dt_{k}\;{\rm g}_{+}(t_{m}-t_{k})\int{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}d\bar{\xi}_{N+1}d\xi_{N+1}^{*}}\prod_{n=0}^{k-1}d^{2}\xi_{n}e^{-\xi_{n}^{*}\xi_{n}}\prod_{n=0}^{m-1}d^{2}\bar{\xi}_{n}e^{-\bar{\xi}_{n}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{n}}

\displaystyle\times{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}d\xi_{m}^{*}}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\prod_{n=m+1}^{N}d\xi_{n-1}d\xi^{*}_{n}}\right){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}d\xi_{N}}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\prod_{n=m+1}^{N}\xi^{*}_{n}\xi_{n-1}p_{n}}\right){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\xi_{N+1}^{*}\xi_{N}p_{N+1}}

\displaystyle\times{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}d\bar{\xi}_{N}^{*}}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\prod_{n=N}^{k+1}d\bar{\xi}_{n}d\bar{\xi}^{*}_{n-1}}\right){\color[rgb]{1,0,0}d\bar{\xi}_{k}}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\prod_{n=k+1}^{N}\bar{\xi}^{*}_{n-1}\bar{\xi}_{n}p^{*}_{n}}\right){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bar{\xi}^{*}_{N}\bar{\xi}_{N+1}p_{N+1}^{*}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\xi_{m}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{k}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{m}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{m}}dt_{k}\;{\rm g}_{+}(t_{m}-t_{k})\int{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}}\prod_{n=0}^{k-1}d^{2}\xi_{n}e^{-\xi_{n}^{*}\xi_{n}}\prod_{n=0}^{m-1}d^{2}\bar{\xi}_{n}e^{-\bar{\xi}_{n}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{n}}

(291)

\displaystyle\times{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}d\xi_{m}^{*}}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\prod_{n=m+1}^{N}d\xi_{n-1}d\xi^{*}_{n}}\right){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}d\xi_{N}d\xi_{N+1}^{*}}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\prod_{n=m+1}^{N}\xi^{*}_{n}\xi_{n-1}p_{n}}\right){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\xi_{N+1}^{*}\xi_{N}p_{N+1}}

\displaystyle\times{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}d\bar{\xi}_{N+1}d\bar{\xi}_{N}^{*}}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\prod_{n=N}^{k+1}d\bar{\xi}_{n}d\bar{\xi}^{*}_{n-1}}\right){\color[rgb]{1,0,0}d\bar{\xi}_{k}}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\prod_{n=k+1}^{N}\bar{\xi}^{*}_{n-1}\bar{\xi}_{n}p^{*}_{n}}\right){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bar{\xi}^{*}_{N}\bar{\xi}_{N+1}p_{N+1}^{*}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\xi_{m}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{k}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{m}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{m}}dt_{k}\;{\rm g}_{+}(t_{m}-t_{k})\prod_{n=m+1}^{N+1}p_{n}\prod_{n=k+1}^{N+1}p_{n}^{*}\int{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}{d\xi_{m}^{*}d\bar{\xi}_{k}\xi_{m}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{k}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{m}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{m}}dt_{k}\;[-{\rm g}_{+}(t_{m}-t_{k})]\prod_{n=k+1}^{m}p_{n}^{*}

\displaystyle\longrightarrow

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{2}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{2}}dt_{2}\;[-{\rm g}_{+}(t_{2}-t_{1})]e^{\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}\epsilon(t_{2}-t_{1})}

We notice that Eqs. (
288
) and (
290
) only differ by a sign and the same holds for Eqs. (
289
) and (
291
).

Now we consider the specific four-transition path (second order in
\Gamma
) individuated by the ordered sequence of Grassmann variables
\{\xi_{k_{1}}^{*}\;,\bar{\xi}_{k_{2}}\;,\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}^{*}\;,\xi_{k_{4}}\}
and shown in Fig.
31
.

Figure 31:
Path with four transitions, two in the forward and the others in the backward branch. Here, only the collective path is represented, with diagonal and off-diagonal states depicted with continuous and dashed lines, respectively. The boxed Grassmann variables in red are the ones appearing in the influence functional in this example.

The contribution of this path to
J^{(2)}_{00}(t;t_{0})
is evaluated along the same lines as above

\displaystyle J^{(2)}_{(e),00}(t;t_{0})=

\displaystyle\Pi_{b}(0)\Pi_{a}^{*}(0)\int d^{2}\xi_{0}d^{2}\bar{\xi}_{0}\mathcal{J}^{(2)}_{(e)}(\xi^{*}_{a},\bar{\xi}_{b},t;\xi_{0},\bar{\xi}^{*}_{0},t_{0})\langle\xi_{0}|0\rangle\langle 0|\bar{\xi}_{0}\rangle

(292)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{k_{4}}\dots\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{k_{2}}}dt_{k_{1}}\;\int d\bar{\xi}_{N+1}\bar{\xi}_{N+1}d\xi_{N+1}^{*}\xi_{N+1}^{*}\prod_{n=0}^{k_{1}-1}d^{2}\xi_{n}e^{-\xi_{n}^{*}\xi_{n}}\prod_{n=k_{4}+1}^{N}d^{2}\xi_{n}e^{-\xi_{n}^{*}\xi_{n}}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{n=0}^{k_{2}-1}d^{2}\bar{\xi}_{n}e^{-\bar{\xi}_{n}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{n}}\prod_{n=k_{3}+1}^{N}d^{2}\bar{\xi}_{n}e^{-\bar{\xi}_{n}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{n}}

\displaystyle\times{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}d\xi_{k_{1}}^{*}}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\prod_{n=k_{1}+1}^{k_{4}}d\xi_{n-1}d\xi^{*}_{n}}\right){\color[rgb]{1,0,0}d\xi_{k_{4}}}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\prod_{n=k_{1}+1}^{k_{4}}\xi^{*}_{n}\xi_{n-1}p_{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\times{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}d\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}^{*}}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\prod_{n=k_{3}}^{k_{2}+1}d\bar{\xi}_{n}d\bar{\xi}^{*}_{n-1}}\right){\color[rgb]{1,0,0}d\bar{\xi}_{k_{2}}}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\prod_{n=k_{2}+1}^{k_{3}}\bar{\xi}^{*}_{n-1}\bar{\xi}_{n}p^{*}_{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\Big\{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bar{\xi}_{k_{2}}}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{*}(t_{k_{2}}-t_{k_{1}})]{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\xi_{k_{1}}^{*}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\xi_{k_{4}}}{\rm g}_{-}^{*}(t_{k_{4}}-t_{k_{3}}){\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}^{*}}

\displaystyle+{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\xi_{k_{4}}}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{*}(t_{k_{4}}-t_{k_{1}})]{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\xi_{k_{1}}^{*}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}^{*}}{\rm g}_{+}(t_{k_{3}}-t_{k_{2}}){\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bar{\xi}_{k_{2}}}\Big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{k_{4}}\dots\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{k_{2}}}dt_{k_{1}}\int d\xi_{k_{1}}^{*}d\xi_{4}d\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}^{*}d\bar{\xi}_{k_{2}}\Big\{\bar{\xi}_{k_{2}}\xi_{k_{1}}^{*}\xi_{k_{4}}\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}^{*}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{*}(t_{k_{2}}-t_{k_{1}})]{\rm g}_{-}^{*}(t_{k_{4}}-t_{k_{3}})

\displaystyle+\xi_{k_{4}}\xi_{k_{1}}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{k_{2}}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{*}(t_{k_{4}}-t_{k_{1}})]{\rm g}_{+}(t_{k_{3}}-t_{k_{2}})\Big\}\prod_{n=k_{1}+1}^{k_{2}}p_{n}\prod_{n=k_{3}+1}^{k_{4}}p_{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{4}\dots\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{2}}dt_{1}\Big\{[-{\rm g}_{+}^{*}(t_{2}-t_{1})][-{\rm g}_{-}^{*}(t_{4}-t_{3})]+[-{\rm g}_{+}^{*}(t_{4}-t_{1})][-{\rm g}_{+}(t_{3}-t_{2})]\Big\}

\displaystyle\times e^{-\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}\epsilon(t_{2}-t_{1})}e^{-\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}\epsilon(t_{4}-t_{3})}\;.

Note that a product of the type
{\rm g}(t_{4}-t_{2}){\rm g}(t_{3}-t_{1})
, implying a crossing of the fermion lines, is not present for this specific path because we have fixed the Grassmann variables at the transition times and the form of the influence functional prevents the fermion lines from joining two starred or two non-starred charges, see Eq. (
6
).

From the examples above we can draw some conclusions

•

Integrations over the
sojourn
time intervals yield an overall phase factor
1
.

•

Integrations over the
blip
time intervals yield the phase factors
\exp(-\zeta{\rm i}\epsilon\tau/\hbar)
, where
\tau
is the blip length and
\zeta=\pm 1
, depending on the nature of the blip.

Once the trivial integrations over the sojourns/blip time intervals are performed, we are left with a final integration over the Grassmann variables associated to the transitions. As a result of this procedure, neither the Grassmann variables in the integration measures nor the ones in the integrands are time-ordered. Specifically,

•

In the integration measure, the backward variables appear to the right of the forward and, within this two classes, starred variables are to the left of the non-starred ones. This reflects the original order of the integrations.

•

In the integrand, the Grassmann variables appear as a sequence of pairs whose order depends on how they are coupled by the functions
{\rm g}(t_{j}-t_{i})
.

SIAM

We now generalize the procedure used for the RLM by analyzing specific instances of paths involving both spin states in the SIAM. To avoid adding further indexes we denote the Grassmann variables associated to
\sigma=\;\uparrow
with the usual
\xi
and the ones associated to
\sigma=\;\downarrow
with the letter
\psi
. As for the RLM, instead of using the collective sojourn index
\boldsymbol{\eta}=(\eta^{\uparrow},\eta^{\downarrow})
, we indicate the initial and final occupation of the spin states of the dot in the argument of the propagator.
The integration measure has the property
d^{2}\boldsymbol{\xi}=d\xi^{*}d\psi^{*}d\xi d\psi=-d\xi^{*}d\xi d\psi^{*}d\psi
, so that
d^{2}\boldsymbol{\xi}d^{2}\bar{\boldsymbol{\xi}}=d\xi^{*}d\xi d\psi^{*}d\psi d\bar{\xi}^{*}d\bar{\xi}d\bar{\psi}^{*}d\bar{\psi}=d^{2}\xi d^{2}\bar{\xi}d^{2}\psi d^{2}\bar{\psi}
. We use this property to factorize the integrations over the Grassmann variables for the two degrees of freedom.

For the SIAM, the coherent states are expressed, in terms of occupation number states, as

\displaystyle|\boldsymbol{\xi}\rangle=

\displaystyle(1-\xi\hat{a}_{\uparrow}^{{\dagger}})(1-\psi\hat{a}_{\downarrow}^{{\dagger}})|0_{\uparrow}0_{\downarrow}\rangle

(293)

\displaystyle=|0_{\uparrow}0_{\downarrow}\rangle+|1_{\uparrow}0_{\downarrow}\rangle\xi+|0_{\uparrow}1_{\downarrow}\rangle\psi+|1_{\uparrow}1_{\downarrow}\rangle\psi\xi

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}|=

\displaystyle\langle 0_{\downarrow}0_{\uparrow}|(1-\hat{a}_{\downarrow}\psi^{*})(1-\hat{a}_{\uparrow}\xi^{*})

\displaystyle=\langle 0_{\downarrow}0_{\uparrow}|+\psi^{*}\langle 1_{\downarrow}0_{\uparrow}|+\xi^{*}\langle 0_{\downarrow}1_{\uparrow}|+\xi^{*}\psi^{*}\langle 1_{\downarrow}1_{\uparrow}|\;.

As a result, the populations are found by calculating the matrix element of the impurity RDM

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}|\rho|\bar{\boldsymbol{\xi}}\rangle={\rm P}_{00}+\dots+\psi^{*}\bar{\psi}\;{\rm P}_{01}+\dots+\xi^{*}\bar{\xi}\;{\rm P}_{10}+\dots+\xi^{*}\psi^{*}\bar{\psi}\bar{\xi}\;{\rm P}_{11}

(294)

and applying the projectors defined in Eq. (
283
) via
{\rm P}_{\boldsymbol{n}}\equiv\rho_{\boldsymbol{n}\boldsymbol{n}}=\Pi_{b}(\boldsymbol{n})\Pi_{a}^{*}(\boldsymbol{n})\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}|\rho|\bar{\boldsymbol{\xi}}\rangle
.

Let us introduce the abbreviations

\displaystyle{\rm overlap}\quad O_{n}^{\uparrow}=

\displaystyle e^{-\xi_{n}^{*}\xi_{n}}\qquad\bar{O}_{n}^{\uparrow}=e^{-\bar{\xi}_{n}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{n}}

(295)

\displaystyle O_{n}^{\downarrow}=

\displaystyle e^{-\psi_{n}^{*}\psi_{n}}\qquad\bar{O}_{n}^{\downarrow}=e^{-\bar{\psi}_{n}^{*}\bar{\psi}_{n}}

\displaystyle H_{0}\quad\mathcal{P}_{n}^{\uparrow}=

\displaystyle e^{\xi_{n}^{*}\xi_{n-1}p_{n}^{\uparrow}}\qquad\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{n}^{\uparrow}=e^{\bar{\xi}_{n-1}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{n}p_{n}^{*\uparrow}}

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{n}^{\downarrow}=

\displaystyle e^{\psi_{n}^{*}\psi_{n-1}p_{n}^{\downarrow}}\qquad\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{n}^{\downarrow}=e^{\bar{\psi}_{n-1}^{*}\bar{\psi}_{n}p_{n}^{*\downarrow}}

\displaystyle{\rm interaction}\quad\mathcal{U}_{n}=

\displaystyle e^{\xi_{n}^{*}\xi_{n-1}\psi_{n}^{*}\psi_{n-1}u_{n}}\qquad\bar{\mathcal{U}}_{n}=e^{\bar{\xi}_{n-1}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{n}\bar{\psi}_{n-1}^{*}\bar{\psi}_{n}u_{n}^{*}}\;,

where
p_{n}^{\sigma}:=1-({\rm i}/\hbar)\epsilon_{\sigma}\delta t_{n}
and
u_{n}:=-({\rm i}/\hbar)U\delta t_{n}
, with
U
the interaction energy.
Consider the path in Fig.
32
. From Eqs. (
282
) and (
283
), the contribution to
J^{(2)}_{10,00}(t;t_{0})
given by the path reads

Figure 32:
Path with four transitions distributed in the two sub-paths of the spin variables
\uparrow
and
\downarrow
. Note that the path for
\sigma=~\uparrow
is of the type (c) in Fig.
30
, while
\sigma=~\downarrow
undergoes a sequence of the type (a) in Fig.
29
.

\displaystyle J^{(2)}_{(f),10,00}(t;t_{0})=

\displaystyle\Pi^{\uparrow}_{b}(1)\Pi^{\downarrow}_{b}(0)\Pi^{\downarrow*}_{a}(0)\Pi^{\uparrow*}_{a}(1)\int d^{2}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{0}d^{2}\bar{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{0}\mathcal{J}^{(2)}_{(f)}(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{*}_{a},\bar{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{b},t;\boldsymbol{\xi}_{0},\bar{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{*}_{0},t_{0})\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}_{0}|\mathbf{0}\rangle\langle\mathbf{0}|\bar{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{0}\rangle

(296)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{k_{4}}\dots\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{k_{2}}}dt_{k_{1}}\;\int d\bar{\xi}_{N+1}d\bar{\psi}_{N+1}\;\bar{\psi}_{N+1}d\psi^{*}_{N+1}\psi^{*}_{N+1}d\xi^{*}_{N+1}\prod_{n=0}^{N}d^{2}\xi_{n}d^{2}\psi_{n}d^{2}\bar{\xi}_{n}d^{2}\bar{\psi}_{n}O_{n}^{\uparrow}O_{n}^{\downarrow}\bar{O}_{n}^{\uparrow}\bar{O}_{n}^{\downarrow}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{n=1}^{N+1}\mathcal{P}_{n}^{\uparrow}\mathcal{P}_{n}^{\downarrow}\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{n}^{\uparrow}\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{n}^{\downarrow}\mathcal{U}_{n}\bar{\mathcal{U}}_{n}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{\uparrow*}(t_{k_{3}}-t_{k_{1}})]{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\xi^{*}_{k_{1}}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\psi_{k_{4}}}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{\downarrow*}(t_{k_{4}}-t_{k_{2}})]{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{k_{4}}\dots\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{k_{2}}}dt_{k_{1}}\;\int d\bar{\xi}_{N+1}d\xi^{*}_{N+1}\left(\prod_{n=0}^{k_{1}-1}d\xi_{n}^{*}d\xi_{n}O_{n}^{\uparrow}\right)d\xi_{k_{1}}^{*}\left(\prod_{n=k_{1}+1}^{N}d\xi_{n-1}d\xi_{n}^{*}\mathcal{P}_{n}^{\uparrow}\right)d\xi_{N}\mathcal{P}_{N+1}^{\uparrow}

\displaystyle\times\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{N+1}^{\uparrow}d\bar{\xi}_{N}^{*}\left(\prod_{n=N}^{k_{3}+1}d\bar{\xi}_{n}d\bar{\xi}_{n-1}^{*}\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{n}^{\uparrow}\right)d\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}\left(\prod_{n=k_{3}-1}^{0}d\bar{\xi}_{n}^{*}d\bar{\xi}_{n}\bar{O}_{n}^{\uparrow}\right){\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{*\uparrow}(t_{k_{3}}-t_{k_{1}})]{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\xi^{*}_{k_{1}}}

\displaystyle\times d\bar{\psi}_{N+1}\;\bar{\psi}_{N+1}d\psi^{*}_{N+1}\psi^{*}_{N+1}\left(\prod_{n=0}^{N}d\bar{\psi}_{n}^{*}d\bar{\psi}_{n}\bar{O_{n}^{\downarrow}}\right)\left(\prod_{n=0}^{k_{2}-1}d\psi_{n}^{*}d\psi_{n}O_{n}^{\downarrow}\right)d\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}

\displaystyle\times\left(\prod_{n=k_{2}+1}^{k_{4}}d\psi_{n-1}d\psi_{n}^{*}\mathcal{P}_{n}^{\downarrow}\mathcal{U}_{n}\right)d\psi_{k_{4}}\left(\prod_{n=k_{4}+1}^{N}d\psi_{n}^{*}d\psi_{n}O_{n}^{\downarrow}\right){\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\psi_{k_{4}}}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{*\downarrow}(t_{k_{4}}-t_{k_{2}})]{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}}\;.

Now, since (see Eq. (
295
))

\displaystyle\prod_{n}d\xi_{n}^{*}d\xi_{n}O_{n}=

\displaystyle\prod_{n}d\xi_{n}^{*}d\xi_{n}e^{-\bar{\xi}_{n}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{n}}=1

(297)

\displaystyle{\rm and}\qquad\prod_{n}d\xi_{n-1}d\xi_{n}^{*}\mathcal{P}_{n}=

\displaystyle\prod_{n}d\xi_{n-1}d\xi_{n}^{*}e^{\xi_{n}^{*}\xi_{n-1}p_{n}}=\prod_{n}p_{n}\;,

we get

\displaystyle J^{(2)}_{(f),10,00}(t;t_{0})=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{k_{4}}\dots\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{k_{2}}}dt_{k_{1}}\;\prod_{n=k_{1}+1}^{N}p_{n}^{\uparrow}\prod_{n=k_{2}+1}^{k_{4}}(p_{n}^{\downarrow}+u_{n})\prod_{n=k_{3}+1}^{N}p_{n}^{\uparrow*}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{\uparrow*}(t_{k_{3}}-t_{k_{1}})][-{\rm g}_{+}^{\downarrow*}(t_{k_{4}}-t_{k_{2}})]

(298)

\displaystyle\times\int d\bar{\xi}_{N+1}d\xi^{*}_{N+1}d\xi_{k_{1}}^{*}d\xi_{N}\mathcal{P}_{N+1}^{\uparrow}\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{N+1}^{\uparrow}d\bar{\xi}_{N}^{*}d\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}\xi^{*}_{k_{1}}

\displaystyle\times d\bar{\psi}_{N+1}\;\bar{\psi}_{N+1}d\psi^{*}_{N+1}\psi^{*}_{N+1}d\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}d\psi_{k_{4}}\psi_{k_{4}}\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{k_{4}}\dots\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{k_{2}}}dt_{k_{1}}\;\prod_{n=k_{1}+1}^{N+1}p_{n}^{\uparrow}\prod_{n=k_{2}+1}^{k_{4}}(p_{n}^{\downarrow}+u_{n})\prod_{n=k_{3}+1}^{N+1}p_{n}^{\uparrow*}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{\uparrow*}(t_{k_{3}}-t_{k_{1}})][-{\rm g}_{+}^{\downarrow*}(t_{k_{4}}-t_{k_{2}})]

\displaystyle\times\int d\xi_{k_{1}}^{*}d\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}\xi^{*}_{k_{1}}d\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}d\psi_{k_{4}}\psi_{k_{4}}\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{4}\dots\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{2}}dt_{1}\;e^{-\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}\epsilon_{\uparrow}(t_{3}-t_{1})}e^{-\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}(\epsilon_{\downarrow}+U)(t_{4}-t_{2})}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{\uparrow*}(t_{3}-t_{1})][-{\rm g}_{+}^{\downarrow*}(t_{4}-t_{2})]\;,

where we have used Eq. (
287
) and the properties that couples of Grassmann variables commute with other Grassmann variables and that variables belonging to different spins anticommute.

Next we consider the process depicted in Fig.

, which contributes to
\rho_{11,11}
. As above, we have

Path with four transitions distributed in the two sub-paths of the spin variables

and

\displaystyle J^{(2)}_{(g)11,00}(t;t_{0})=

\displaystyle\Pi^{\uparrow}_{b}(1)\Pi^{\downarrow}_{b}(1)\Pi^{\downarrow*}_{a}(1)\Pi^{\uparrow*}_{a}(1)\int d^{2}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{0}d^{2}\bar{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{0}\mathcal{J}^{(2)}_{(g)}(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{*}_{a},\bar{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{b},t;\boldsymbol{\xi}_{0},\bar{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{*}_{0},t_{0})\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}_{0}|\mathbf{0}\rangle\langle\mathbf{0}|\bar{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{0}\rangle

(299)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{k_{4}}\dots\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{k_{2}}}dt_{k_{1}}\;\int d\bar{\xi}_{N+1}d\bar{\psi}_{N+1}\;d\psi^{*}_{N+1}d\xi^{*}_{N+1}\prod_{n=0}^{N}d^{2}\xi_{n}d^{2}\psi_{n}d^{2}\bar{\xi}_{n}d^{2}\bar{\psi}_{n}O_{n}^{\uparrow}O_{n}^{\downarrow}\bar{O}_{n}^{\uparrow}\bar{O}_{n}^{\downarrow}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{n=1}^{N+1}\mathcal{P}_{n}^{\uparrow}\mathcal{P}_{n}^{\downarrow}\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{n}^{\uparrow}\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{n}^{\downarrow}\mathcal{U}_{n}\bar{\mathcal{U}}_{n}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{\uparrow*}(t_{k_{3}}-t_{k_{1}})]{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\xi^{*}_{k_{1}}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bar{\psi}_{k_{4}}}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{\downarrow*}(t_{k_{4}}-t_{k_{2}})]{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{k_{4}}\dots\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{k_{2}}}dt_{k_{1}}\;\int d\bar{\xi}_{N+1}d\xi^{*}_{N+1}\left(\prod_{n=0}^{k_{1}-1}d\xi_{n}^{*}d\xi_{n}O_{n}^{\uparrow}\right)d\xi_{k_{1}}^{*}\left(\prod_{n=k_{1}+1}^{N}d\xi_{n-1}d\xi_{n}^{*}\mathcal{P}_{n}^{\uparrow}\right)d\xi_{N}\mathcal{P}_{N+1}^{\uparrow}

\displaystyle\times\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{N+1}^{\uparrow}d\bar{\xi}_{N}^{*}\left(\prod_{n=N}^{k_{3}+1}d\bar{\xi}_{n}d\bar{\xi}_{n-1}^{*}\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{n}^{\uparrow}\right)d\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}\left(\prod_{n=k_{3}-1}^{0}d\bar{\xi}_{n}^{*}d\bar{\xi}_{n}\bar{O}_{n}^{\uparrow}\right){\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{\uparrow*}(t_{k_{3}}-t_{k_{1}})]{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\xi^{*}_{k_{1}}}

\displaystyle\times d\bar{\psi}_{N+1}d\psi^{*}_{N+1}\left(\prod_{n=0}^{k_{2}-1}d\psi_{n}^{*}d\psi_{n}O_{n}^{\downarrow}\right)d\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}\left(\prod_{n=k_{2}+1}^{N}d\psi_{n-1}d\psi_{n}^{*}\mathcal{P}_{n}^{\downarrow}\mathcal{U}_{n}\right)d\psi_{N}\mathcal{P}_{N+1}^{\downarrow}\mathcal{U}_{N+1}

\displaystyle\times\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{N+1}^{\downarrow}\bar{\mathcal{U}}_{N+1}d\bar{\psi}_{N}^{*}\left(\prod_{n=N}^{k_{4}+1}d\bar{\psi}_{n}d\bar{\psi}_{n-1}^{*}\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{n}^{\downarrow}\bar{\mathcal{U}}_{n}\right)d\bar{\psi}_{k_{4}}\left(\prod_{n=k_{4}-1}^{0}d\bar{\psi}_{n}^{*}d\bar{\psi}_{n}\bar{O}_{n}^{\downarrow}\right){\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bar{\psi}_{k_{4}}}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{\downarrow*}(t_{k_{4}}-t_{k_{2}})]{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}}\;.

Again we use the definitions of
\mathcal{P}_{n}
and
\mathcal{O}_{n}
to integrate out the terms in parenthesis and get

\displaystyle J^{(2)}_{(g)11,00}(t;t_{0})=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{k_{4}}\dots\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{k_{2}}}dt_{k_{1}}\;\prod_{n=k_{1}+1}^{N}p_{n}^{\uparrow}\prod_{n=k_{2}+1}^{N}(p_{n}^{\downarrow}+u_{n})\prod_{n=k_{3}+1}^{N}p_{n}^{\uparrow*}\prod_{n=k_{4}+1}^{N}(p_{n}^{\downarrow*}+u_{n}^{*})

(300)

\displaystyle\times[-{\rm g}_{+}^{\uparrow*}(t_{k_{3}}-t_{k_{1}})][-{\rm g}_{+}^{\downarrow*}(t_{k_{4}}-t_{k_{2}})]

\displaystyle\times\int d\bar{\xi}_{N+1}d\xi^{*}_{N+1}d\xi_{k_{1}}^{*}d\xi_{N}\mathcal{P}_{N+1}^{\uparrow}\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{N+1}^{\uparrow}d\bar{\xi}_{N}^{*}d\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}\xi^{*}_{k_{1}}

\displaystyle\times d\bar{\psi}_{N+1}d\psi^{*}_{N+1}d\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}d\psi_{N}\mathcal{P}_{N+1}^{\downarrow}\mathcal{U}_{N+1}\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{N+1}^{\downarrow}\bar{\mathcal{U}}_{N+1}d\bar{\psi}_{N}^{*}d\bar{\psi}_{k_{4}}\bar{\psi}_{k_{4}}\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{k_{4}}\dots\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{k_{2}}}dt_{k_{1}}\;\prod_{n=k_{1}+1}^{N+1}p_{n}^{\uparrow}\prod_{n=k_{2}+1}^{N+1}(p_{n}^{\downarrow}+u_{n})\prod_{n=k_{3}+1}^{N+1}p_{n}^{\uparrow*}\prod_{n=k_{4}+1}^{N+1}(p_{n}^{\downarrow*}+u_{n}^{*})

\displaystyle\times[-{\rm g}_{+}^{\uparrow*}(t_{k_{3}}-t_{k_{1}})][-{\rm g}_{+}^{\downarrow*}(t_{k_{4}}-t_{k_{2}})]\int d\xi_{k_{1}}^{*}d\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}\bar{\xi}_{k_{3}}\xi^{*}_{k_{1}}d\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}d\bar{\psi}_{k_{4}}\bar{\psi}_{k_{4}}\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{4}\dots\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{2}}dt_{1}\;e^{-\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}\epsilon_{\uparrow}(t_{3}-t_{1})}e^{-\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}(\epsilon_{\downarrow}+U)(t_{4}-t_{2})}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{\uparrow*}(t_{3}-t_{1})][-{\rm g}_{+}^{\downarrow*}(t_{4}-t_{2})]\;.

It is already apparent that the interaction is present in the time intervals where both spin states are occupied either in the forward or in the backward branch. Simultaneous double occupation in the two branches, as it is the case for the sojourn states (11,11), leads to a cancellation due to the sum of
u_{n}
and
u_{n}^{*}
at the exponent.

Figure 34:
Path with four transitions distributed in the two sub-paths of the spin variables
\uparrow
and
\downarrow
.
Note that the sub-path
\sigma=~\uparrow
is of the type (b) in Fig.
29
, while
\sigma=~\downarrow
undergoes a sequence of the type (c), see Fig.
30
.

To better clarify this point, consider the example in Fig. (
34
). This path contributes to
J^{(2)}_{01,00}(t;t_{0})
, the contribution being

\displaystyle J^{(2)}_{(h)01,00}(t;t_{0})=

\displaystyle\Pi^{\uparrow}_{b}(0)\Pi^{\downarrow}_{b}(1)\Pi^{\downarrow*}_{a}(1)\Pi^{\uparrow*}_{a}(0)\int d^{2}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{0}d^{2}\bar{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{0}\mathcal{J}^{(2)}_{(h)}(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{*}_{a},\bar{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{b},t;\boldsymbol{\xi}_{0},\bar{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{*}_{0},t_{0})\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}_{0}|\mathbf{0}\rangle\langle\mathbf{0}|\bar{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{0}\rangle

(301)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{k_{4}}\dots\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{k_{2}}}dt_{k_{1}}\;\int d\bar{\xi}_{N+1}\bar{\xi}_{N+1}d\bar{\psi}_{N+1}\;d\psi^{*}_{N+1}d\xi^{*}_{N+1}\xi^{*}_{N+1}\;\prod_{n=0}^{N}d^{2}\xi_{n}d^{2}\psi_{n}d^{2}\bar{\xi}_{n}d^{2}\bar{\psi}_{n}O_{n}^{\uparrow}O_{n}^{\downarrow}\bar{O}_{n}^{\uparrow}\bar{O}_{n}^{\downarrow}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{n=1}^{N+1}\mathcal{P}_{n}^{\uparrow}\mathcal{P}_{n}^{\downarrow}\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{n}^{\uparrow}\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{n}^{\downarrow}\mathcal{U}_{n}\bar{\mathcal{U}}_{n}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bar{\xi}_{k_{4}}^{*}}{\rm g}_{+}^{\uparrow}(t_{k_{4}}-t_{k_{1}}){\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bar{\xi}_{k_{1}}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bar{\psi}_{k_{3}}}[-1{\rm g}_{+}^{\downarrow*}(t_{k_{3}}-t_{k_{2}})]{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{k_{4}}\dots\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{k_{2}}}dt_{k_{1}}\;\int d\xi^{*}_{N+1}d\bar{\xi}_{N+1}\bar{\xi}_{N+1}\xi^{*}_{N+1}\left(\prod_{n=0}^{N}d\xi_{n}^{*}d\xi_{n}O_{n}^{\uparrow}\right)\left(\prod_{n=N}^{k_{4}+1}d\bar{\xi}_{n}^{*}d\bar{\xi}_{n}\bar{O}_{n}^{\uparrow}\right)d\bar{\xi}_{k_{4}}^{*}

\displaystyle\times\left(\prod_{n=k_{4}}^{k_{1}+1}d\bar{\xi}_{n}d\bar{\xi}_{n-1}^{*}\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{n}^{\uparrow}\right)d\bar{\xi}_{1}\left(\prod_{n=k_{1}-1}^{0}d\bar{\xi}_{n}^{*}d\bar{\xi}_{n}\bar{O}_{n}^{\uparrow}\right){\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bar{\xi}_{k_{4}}^{*}}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{\uparrow}(t_{k_{4}}-t_{k_{1}})]{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bar{\xi}_{k_{1}}}

\displaystyle\times d\psi^{*}_{N+1}d\bar{\psi}_{N+1}\left(\prod_{n=0}^{k_{2}-1}d\psi_{n}^{*}d\psi_{n}O_{n}^{\downarrow}\right)d\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}\left(\prod_{n=k_{2}+1}^{N}d\psi_{n-1}d\psi_{n}^{*}\mathcal{P}_{n}^{\downarrow}\right)d\psi_{N}\mathcal{P}_{N+1}^{\downarrow}

\displaystyle\times d\bar{\psi}_{N}^{*}\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{N+1}^{\downarrow}\left(\prod_{n=N}^{k_{3}+1}d\bar{\psi}_{n}d\bar{\psi}_{n-1}^{*}\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{n}^{\downarrow}\right)d\bar{\psi}_{k_{3}}\left(\prod_{n=k_{3}-1}^{0}d\bar{\psi}_{n}^{*}d\bar{\psi}_{n}\bar{O}_{n}^{\downarrow}\right)\prod_{n=k_{3}+1}^{k_{4}}\bar{\mathcal{U}}_{n}

\displaystyle\times{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bar{\psi}_{k_{3}}}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{\downarrow*}(t_{k_{3}}-t_{k_{2}})]{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}}\;.

Once the trivial integrations are carried out as before, we are left with

\displaystyle J^{(2)}_{(h)01,00}(t;t_{0})=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{k_{4}}\dots\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{k_{2}}}dt_{k_{1}}\;\prod_{n=k_{2}+1}^{N}p_{n}^{\downarrow}\prod_{n=k_{1}+1}^{k_{4}}p_{n}^{\uparrow*}\prod_{n=k_{3}+1}^{k_{4}}(p_{n}^{\downarrow*}+u_{n}^{*})\prod_{n=k_{4}+1}^{N}p_{n}^{\downarrow*}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{\uparrow}(t_{k_{4}}-t_{k_{1}})][-{\rm g}_{+}^{\downarrow*}(t_{k_{3}}-t_{k_{2}})]

(302)

\displaystyle\times\int d\xi^{*}_{N+1}d\bar{\xi}_{N+1}\bar{\xi}_{N+1}\xi^{*}_{N+1}d\bar{\xi}_{k_{4}}^{*}d\bar{\xi}_{1}\bar{\xi}_{k_{4}}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{k_{1}}d\psi^{*}_{N+1}d\bar{\psi}_{N+1}d\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}d\psi_{N}\mathcal{P}_{N+1}^{\downarrow}d\bar{\psi}_{N}^{*}\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{N+1}^{\downarrow}d\bar{\psi}_{k_{3}}\bar{\psi}_{k_{3}}\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{k_{4}}\dots\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{k_{2}}}dt_{k_{1}}\;\prod_{n=k_{2}+1}^{N+1}p_{n}^{\downarrow}\prod_{n=k_{1}+1}^{k_{4}}p_{n}^{\uparrow*}\prod_{n=k_{3}+1}^{k_{4}}(p_{n}^{\downarrow*}+u_{n}^{*})\prod_{n=k_{4}+1}^{N+1}p_{n}^{\downarrow*}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{\uparrow}(t_{k_{4}}-t_{k_{1}})][-{\rm g}_{+}^{\downarrow*}(t_{k_{3}}-t_{k_{2}})]

\displaystyle\times\int d\bar{\xi}_{k_{4}}^{*}d\bar{\xi}_{1}\bar{\xi}_{k_{4}}^{*}\bar{\xi}_{k_{1}}d\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}d\bar{\psi}_{k_{3}}\bar{\psi}_{k_{3}}\psi_{k_{2}}^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{0}}^{t}dt_{4}\dots\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{2}}dt_{1}\;e^{\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}\epsilon_{\uparrow}(t_{4}-t_{1})}e^{-\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}\epsilon_{\downarrow}(t_{3}-t_{2})}e^{\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}U(t_{4}-t_{3})}[-{\rm g}_{+}^{\uparrow}(t_{4}-t_{1})][-{\rm g}_{+}^{\downarrow*}(t_{3}-t_{2})]\;.

In the above examples the phase factors stemming from the action of the dot have been factorized as
\prod_{j=1}^{n}\exp[-\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}E_{j}\tau_{j}]
in order to reflect the time intervals
\tau_{j}
between transitions. One can recognize that these phase factors are related to the blip/sojourn states of the underlying spin paths according to the example in Fig.
7
.

\int\mathcal{D}\{\xi\}

(b).

Figure 35:
Six-transition path. General case (a) and two specific examples: In the first
\eta_{2}
is left unspecified while
\eta_{0}=\eta_{1}=\eta_{3}=-1
and
\zeta_{1}=\zeta_{2}=\zeta_{3}=+1
(b). In the second also
\zeta_{3}
is left unspecified (c).

Following Eq. (
46
), the integration measure reads

\displaystyle

\displaystyle-\eta_{2}d\xi_{1}^{*}d\xi_{2}d\xi_{3}^{*}(d{\xi_{4}})_{-\eta_{2}}(d{\xi_{5}^{*}})_{-\eta_{2}}d\xi_{6}

(303)

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}d\xi_{1}^{*}d\xi_{2}d\xi_{3}^{*}d{\xi_{4}}d{\xi_{5}^{*}}d\xi_{6}\;,\quad\eta_{2}=-1\\
d\xi_{1}^{*}d\xi_{2}d\xi_{3}^{*}d\xi_{6}d\bar{\xi}_{5}^{*}d\bar{\xi}_{4}\;,\quad\eta_{2}=+1\end{cases}

As a further example, we leave also
\zeta_{3}
unspecified, see Fig.
35
(c), so that the integration measure reads

\displaystyle

\displaystyle-\eta_{2}\zeta_{3}d\xi_{1}^{*}d\xi_{2}d\xi_{3}^{*}(d{\xi_{4}})_{-\eta_{2}}(d{\xi_{5}})^{-\xi_{3}}_{-\eta_{2}\zeta_{3}}(d{\xi_{6}})^{\zeta_{3}}_{\zeta_{3}}

(304)

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}d\xi_{1}^{*}d\xi_{2}d\xi_{3}^{*}d{\xi_{4}}d{\xi_{5}^{*}}d\xi_{6}\;,\quad\eta_{2}=-1,\zeta_{3}=+1\\
d\xi_{1}^{*}d\xi_{2}d\xi_{3}^{*}d\xi_{6}d\bar{\xi}_{5}^{*}d\bar{\xi}_{4}\;,\quad\eta_{2}=+1,\zeta_{3}=+1\\
d\xi_{1}^{*}d\xi_{2}d\xi_{3}^{*}d\xi_{4}d\bar{\xi}_{6}^{*}d\bar{\xi}_{5}\;,\quad\eta_{2}=-1,\zeta_{3}=-1\\
d\xi_{1}^{*}d\xi_{2}d\xi_{3}^{*}d\xi_{5}d\bar{\xi}_{6}^{*}d\bar{\xi}_{4}\;,\quad\eta_{2}=+1,\zeta_{3}=-1\\
\end{cases}

Carrying out an integration over the Grassmann variables associated to the tunneling transitions is straightforward in the present derivation: The integral yields simply an overall sign, due to the order of the variables to be integrated, times the factors
(-\eta_{k}\zeta_{k})
.

\displaystyle=-\eta^{\prime}\eta\eta_{1}\eta_{2}[-{\rm g}_{-\eta}^{-\zeta_{1}}(4,1)]{\rm b}_{41}\;[-{\rm g}_{\eta_{1}}^{\zeta_{1}}(5,2)]{\rm b}_{52}\;[-{\rm g}_{-\eta_{1}}^{-\zeta_{1}}(6,3)]{\rm b}_{63}\delta_{\zeta_{3},\zeta_{1}}\delta_{\zeta_{2},\zeta_{1}}\;.

(309)

\displaystyle(+1)\prod_{k=1}^{4}(-\zeta_{k}\eta_{k})\;[\eta_{0}\zeta_{1}{\rm g}_{-\eta_{0}}^{-\zeta_{1}}(8,1)]{\rm b}_{81}\;[\zeta_{1}\eta_{1}{\rm g}_{\eta_{1}}^{\zeta_{1}}(7,2)]{\rm b}_{72}\;[\eta_{1}\zeta_{2}{\rm g}_{-\eta_{1}}^{-\zeta_{2}}(4,3)]{\rm b}_{43}\;[\eta_{2}\zeta_{3}{\rm g}_{-\eta_{2}}^{-\zeta_{3}}(6,5)]{\rm b}_{65}\;\delta_{\zeta_{4},\zeta_{1}}

\displaystyle=\eta^{\prime}\eta\eta_{1}\eta_{3}\;[-{\rm g}_{-\eta}^{-\zeta_{1}}(8,1)]{\rm b}_{81}\;[-{\rm g}_{\eta_{1}}^{\zeta_{1}}(7,2)]{\rm b}_{72}\;[-{\rm g}_{-\eta_{1}}^{-\zeta_{2}}(4,3)]{\rm b}_{43}\;[-{\rm g}_{-\eta_{2}}^{-\zeta_{3}}(6,5)]{\rm b}_{65}\;\delta_{\zeta_{4},\zeta_{1}}\;.

Appendix I
Contraction integrals in the wide-band limit

•

Integral not involving the Fermi function (
\zeta=\pm 1
)

\displaystyle I_{0}(\mathcal{E};\zeta)=

\displaystyle\int_{-W}^{W}dx\frac{1}{x-\mathcal{E}^{\prime}+{\rm i}\zeta\;\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}}

(310)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle-{\rm i}\zeta\pi\qquad\qquad(W\gg\mathcal{E}^{\prime})\,.

•

Integral involving
f_{-\eta}(x)
,where
\eta=\pm 1
, with
f_{+}(x)=[e^{\beta(x-\mu)}+1]^{-1}
the Fermi function and
f_{-}(x)=1-f_{+}(x)
. Assume
\mathcal{E}
independent of
x
, with
\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}>0
, and
{W}\gg{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime},\mu

\displaystyle I_{+}(\mathcal{E})=

\displaystyle\int_{-W}^{W}d\epsilon\frac{f_{+}(\epsilon)}{\epsilon-\mathcal{E}^{\prime}+{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}}

(311)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{-\bar{W}}^{\bar{W}}dx\frac{\bar{f}_{+}(x)}{x-(\mathcal{E}^{\prime}-\mu)/k_{\rm B}T+{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}/k_{\rm B}T}\qquad\qquad[\bar{f}_{+}(x)=(e^{x}+1)^{-1},\qquad\bar{W}\simeq W/{k_{\rm B}T}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\pi{\rm i}\sum_{j}{\rm Res}_{j}\left\{\frac{\bar{f}_{+}(z)}{z-(\mathcal{E}^{\prime}-\mu)/k_{\rm B}T+{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}/k_{\rm B}T}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\pi{\rm i}\sum_{k=0}^{k_{\bar{W}}}\frac{1}{2\pi{\rm i}(k+1/2)-(\mathcal{E}^{\prime}-\mu)/k_{\rm B}T+{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}/k_{\rm B}T}-{\rm i}\frac{\pi}{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{k=0}^{k_{\bar{W}}}\frac{1}{k+1/2+\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}/(2\pi k_{\rm B}T)+{\rm i}(\mathcal{E}^{\prime}-\mu)/(2\pi k_{\rm B}T)}-{\rm i}\frac{\pi}{2}\;.

Now, since
W\rightarrow\infty
, the sum can be extended to infinity. Following Ref.
Leijnse and Wegewijs 2008
, if
\mathcal{E}
is independent of
\epsilon
, we single out the
k=0
term in the sum over
k
and add and subtract the Euler-Mascheroni constant
\gamma_{E}=\lim_{K\to\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{K}1/k-\ln(K)
. At this point, using the definition of digamma function
\psi(z)=-\gamma_{E}-1/z-\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}[1/(k+z)-1/k]
, we obtain

\displaystyle I_{+}(\mathcal{E})=

\displaystyle\rm{\displaystyle Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{\prime}-{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-\ln\frac{W}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}-{\rm i}\left[\frac{\pi}{2}-{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{\prime}-{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\right]

(312)

\displaystyle I_{-}(\mathcal{E})=

\displaystyle\int_{-W}^{W}dx\frac{f_{-}(x)}{x-\mathcal{E}^{\prime}+{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}}=\int_{-W}^{W}dx\frac{1-f_{+}(x)}{x-\mathcal{E}^{\prime}+{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{\prime}-{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)+\ln\frac{W}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}-{\rm i}\left[\frac{\pi}{2}+{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{\prime}-{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\right]\;,

where we used Eq. (
310
). Thus

\displaystyle I(\mathcal{E};\eta)=

\displaystyle\int_{-W}^{W}dx\frac{f_{-\eta}(x)}{x-\mathcal{E}^{\prime}+{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}}

(313)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\eta\left[{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{\prime}-{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-\ln\frac{W}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right]-{\rm i}\left[\frac{\pi}{2}+\eta{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{\prime}-{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\right]

Further

\displaystyle I^{*}(\mathcal{E};\eta)=

\displaystyle\int_{-W}^{W}dx\frac{f_{-\eta}(x)}{x-\mathcal{E}^{\prime}-{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}}

(314)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\eta\left[{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{\prime}-{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-\ln\frac{W}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right]+{\rm i}\left[\frac{\pi}{2}+\eta{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{\prime}-{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\right]

Then, collecting the above results we can give the
compact expression

\displaystyle I(\mathcal{E};\zeta,\eta)=

\displaystyle\int_{-W}^{W}dx\frac{f_{-\eta}(x)}{x-\mathcal{E}^{\prime}+{\rm i}\zeta\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}}

(315)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\eta\left[{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{\prime}-{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-\ln\frac{W}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right]-{\rm i}\zeta\left[\frac{\pi}{2}+\eta{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{\prime}-{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\right]

•

Special case:
\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}=0^{+}
.

\displaystyle\int_{-W}^{W}dx\frac{f_{-\eta}(x)}{x-\mathcal{E}^{\prime}+{\rm i}\zeta\;0^{+}}=-\eta\left[{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{\prime}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-\ln\frac{W}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right]-{\rm i}\zeta\pi f_{-\eta}(\mathcal{E}^{\prime})\;,

(316)

see also Eq. (E1) of Ref.
Leijnse and Wegewijs 2008
. Here we used the property

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\mp\frac{1}{\pi}{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{\prime}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)=f_{\pm}(\mathcal{E}^{\prime})

(317)

and also
f_{-\eta}(x)=\delta_{\eta,+1}-\eta f_{+}(x)
.
Note that

\int_{-W}^{W}dx\frac{1}{x-\mathcal{E}^{\prime}+{\rm i}\zeta\;0^{+}}=\sum_{\eta}\int_{-W}^{W}dx\frac{f_{-\eta}(x)}{x-\mathcal{E}^{\prime}+{\rm i}\zeta\;0^{+}}=-{\rm i}\zeta\pi\;,

in agreement with Eq. (
310
).

•

We assume that, in general,
\mathcal{E}=\mathcal{E}(\zeta,\eta)
and consider the distinct cases
\mathcal{E}(\eta)=\mathcal{E}_{\eta}
and
\mathcal{E}(\zeta)=\mathcal{E}_{\zeta}
. In the first case, summing over
\eta

\displaystyle\sum_{\eta}I(\mathcal{E}_{\eta};\zeta,\eta)=

\displaystyle-\left[{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{\prime}_{+}-{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}_{+}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{\prime}_{-}-{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}_{-}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\right]

(318)

\displaystyle-{\rm i}\zeta\left[\pi+{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{\prime}_{+}-{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}_{+}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{\prime}_{-}-{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}_{-}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\right]\;.

Note that if
\mathcal{E}
is independent of
\eta
, then
\sum_{\eta}I(\mathcal{E};\zeta,\eta)=-{\rm i}\zeta\pi
. Likewise, when
\mathcal{E}=\mathcal{E}_{\zeta}

\displaystyle\sum_{\zeta}\zeta\;I(\mathcal{E}_{\zeta};\zeta,\eta)=

\displaystyle-\eta\left[{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{\prime}_{+}-{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}_{+}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{\prime}_{-}-{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}_{-}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\right]

(319)

\displaystyle-{\rm i}\left[\pi+\eta{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{\prime}_{+}-{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}_{+}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)+\eta{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{\prime}_{-}-{\rm i}\mathcal{E}^{\prime\prime}_{-}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\right]\;.

If
\mathcal{E}
is independent of
\zeta
, then
\sum_{\zeta}\zeta I(\mathcal{E};\zeta,\eta)=2{\rm i}\zeta{\rm Im}I(\mathcal{E};\zeta,\eta)
.

Appendix J
Evaluation of the NCA4 fourth-tier bubbles
\mathbf{B}_{4}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}
and
\mathbf{B}_{4}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}

The fourth-tier bubbles
{\rm B}_{4,\nu^{\prime}\nu}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}
and
{\rm B}_{4,\eta^{\prime}\eta}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}
have the same structure as the NCA2 second-tier bubbles
{\rm B}^{\bar{\sigma}}_{\nu^{\prime}\nu}
and
{\rm B}^{\sigma}_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}
[Eq. (
168
)], respectively, except for the additional upper layers of fermion lines, and the products of sojourn indexes associated to the overlap of three fermion lines of the same spin. They are schematized as (in view of calculating the retarded self-energy we consider
\zeta=+1
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{3}}\frac{{\rm i}\hbar\,\sum_{\eta}{\rm v}_{\eta}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{3})\delta_{\zeta_{3},-\zeta_{2}}}{\zeta(\epsilon_{k}-\epsilon_{k_{1}})+\zeta_{2}(\epsilon_{k_{2}}-\epsilon_{k_{3}})+{\rm i}0^{+}}\delta_{\nu^{\prime}\nu}\Big|_{\zeta=+1}+\sum_{\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{3}}\frac{\nu^{\prime}\nu\;{\rm i}\hbar\,{\rm v}_{-\nu}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{3})}{\zeta(\epsilon_{k}-\epsilon_{k_{1}})+\zeta_{2}(\epsilon_{k_{2}}-E_{\bar{\sigma}})+\zeta_{3}(\epsilon_{k_{3}}-E_{\sigma})+{\rm i}0^{+}}\Big|_{\zeta=+1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{3}}\frac{-{\rm i}\zeta_{2}\hbar\,{\rm v}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{3})\delta_{\zeta_{3},-\zeta_{2}}}{\epsilon_{k_{3}}-\epsilon_{k_{2}}-\zeta_{2}(\epsilon_{k}-\epsilon_{k_{1}})-{\rm i}\zeta_{2}0^{+}}\delta_{\nu^{\prime}\nu}+\sum_{\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{3}}\frac{\nu^{\prime}\nu\;{\rm i}\zeta_{3}\hbar\,{\rm v}_{-\nu}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{3})}{\epsilon_{k_{3}}-E_{\sigma}+\zeta_{3}(\epsilon_{k}-\epsilon_{k_{1}})+\zeta_{3}\zeta_{2}(\epsilon_{k_{2}}-E_{\bar{\sigma}})+{\rm i}\zeta_{3}0^{+}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\zeta_{2}\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}\sum_{\alpha}\varrho_{\alpha}|{\rm t}_{\alpha}|^{2}\int d\epsilon_{3}\;\frac{\delta_{\zeta_{3},-\zeta_{2}}}{\epsilon_{3}-\mathcal{E}-{\rm i}\zeta_{2}0^{+}}\delta_{\nu^{\prime}\nu}-\nu^{\prime}\nu\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}\sum_{\zeta_{3}}\zeta_{3}\sum_{\alpha}\frac{\Gamma_{\alpha}}{2\pi}\int_{-W}^{W}d\epsilon_{3}\;\frac{f^{\alpha}_{-\nu}(\epsilon_{3})}{\epsilon_{3}-\mathcal{E}_{\sigma,\zeta_{3}}^{(4)}+{\rm i}\zeta_{3}0^{+}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\Gamma}{2\hbar}\delta_{\nu^{\prime}\nu}-\nu^{\prime}\nu\sum_{\alpha}\frac{\Gamma_{\alpha}}{2\hbar}\left\{f^{\alpha}_{-\nu}(\mathcal{E}_{\sigma,+}^{(4)})+f^{\alpha}_{-\nu}(\mathcal{E}_{\sigma,-}^{(4)})-\frac{{\rm i}\nu}{\pi}\Bigg[{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}_{\sigma,+}^{(4)}-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}_{\sigma,-}^{(4)}-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg]\right\}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle-\frac{\Gamma}{2\hbar}\delta_{\nu^{\prime}\nu}-\nu^{\prime}\nu\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}\Sigma_{4,\sigma\nu}^{(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}\;,

where
E_{\sigma}=\epsilon_{\sigma}+U/2
and
{\rm v}_{x}(\kappa):=-(|{\rm t}_{\alpha}(\epsilon_{k})|^{2}/\hbar^{2})f^{\alpha}_{x}(\epsilon)
(note the product
\nu^{\prime}\nu
given by the overlap of three fermion lines with spin
\sigma
). Here we used the property (
317
) to define the fourth-tier self-energy

\Sigma_{4,\sigma\nu}^{(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}:=-\nu\sum_{\alpha}\frac{\Gamma_{\alpha}}{2\pi}\Bigg[\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}_{\sigma,+}^{(4)}-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-\psi^{*}\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}_{\sigma,-}^{(4)}-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg]-{\rm i}\frac{\Gamma}{2}\;,

(321)

where

\mathcal{E}_{\sigma,\pm}^{(4)}:=\epsilon_{\sigma}\pm\zeta(\epsilon_{1}-\epsilon)\pm\zeta_{2}(\epsilon_{\bar{\sigma}}-\epsilon_{2})+\delta_{\zeta_{2},\pm 1}U\;.

(322)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{3}}\frac{{\rm i}\hbar\,\sum_{\nu}{\rm v}_{\nu}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{3})\delta_{\zeta_{3},-\zeta_{2}}}{\zeta(\epsilon_{k}-\epsilon_{k_{1}})+\zeta_{2}(\epsilon_{k_{2}}-\epsilon_{k_{3}})+{\rm i}0^{+}}\delta_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}+\sum_{\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{3}}\frac{{\rm i}\hbar\,{\rm v}_{-\eta}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{3})}{\zeta(\epsilon_{k}-\epsilon_{k_{1}})+\zeta_{2}(\epsilon_{k_{2}}-E_{\sigma})+\zeta_{3}(\epsilon_{k_{3}}-E_{\bar{\sigma}})+{\rm i}0^{+}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{3}}\frac{-{\rm i}\zeta_{2}\hbar\,{\rm v}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{3})\delta_{\zeta_{3},-\zeta_{2}}}{\epsilon_{k_{3}}-\epsilon_{k_{2}}-\zeta_{2}\zeta(\epsilon_{k}-\epsilon_{k_{1}})-{\rm i}\zeta_{2}0^{+}}\delta_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}+\sum_{\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{3}}\frac{{\rm i}\zeta_{3}\hbar\,{\rm v}_{-\eta}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{3})}{\epsilon_{k_{3}}-E_{\bar{\sigma}}+\zeta_{3}\zeta(\epsilon_{k}-\epsilon_{k_{1}})+\zeta_{3}\zeta_{2}(\epsilon_{k_{2}}-E_{\sigma})+{\rm i}\zeta_{3}0^{+}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\zeta_{2}\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}\sum_{\alpha}\varrho_{\alpha}|{\rm t}_{\alpha}|^{2}\int d\epsilon_{3}\;\frac{\delta_{\zeta_{3},-\zeta_{2}}}{\epsilon_{3}-\mathcal{E}-{\rm i}\zeta_{2}0^{+}}\delta_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}-\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}\sum_{\zeta_{3}}\zeta_{3}\sum_{\alpha}\frac{\Gamma_{\alpha}}{2\pi}\int_{-W}^{W}d\epsilon_{3}\;\frac{f^{\alpha}_{-\eta}(\epsilon_{3})}{\epsilon_{3}-\mathcal{E}_{\bar{\sigma},\zeta_{3}}+{\rm i}\zeta_{3}0^{+}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\Gamma}{2\hbar}\delta_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}-\sum_{\alpha}\frac{\Gamma_{\alpha}}{2\hbar}\left\{f^{\alpha}_{-\eta}(\mathcal{E}_{\bar{\sigma},+})+f^{\alpha}_{-\eta}(\mathcal{E}_{\bar{\sigma},-})-\frac{{\rm i}\eta}{\pi}\Bigg[{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}_{\bar{\sigma},+}-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\mathcal{E}_{\bar{\sigma},-}-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg]\right\}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle-\frac{\Gamma}{2\hbar}\delta_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}-\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}\Sigma_{4,\sigma\eta}^{(\sigma\sigma)}\;,

where
\Sigma_{4,\sigma\eta}^{(\sigma\sigma)}
is defined as in Eq. (
321
), with
\nu\rightarrow\eta
and
\sigma\rightarrow\bar{\sigma}
. Note that the 4th-tier self-energies
\Sigma_{4,\sigma}
are formally identical to the 2nd-tier NCA2 self-energies, see Eq. (
192
).

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{2}^{\sigma(\sigma)}=\left[[\mathbf{1}{\rm h}_{2}^{\sigma(\sigma)}]^{-1}-\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{\sigma(\sigma)}\right]^{-1}\;,\qquad{\rm where}\quad{\rm h}_{2}^{\sigma(\sigma)}={\rm i}\hbar\frac{\delta_{\zeta_{1},-\zeta}}{\zeta(\epsilon_{k}-\epsilon_{k_{1}})+{\rm i}0^{+}}\;,

}\end{gathered}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\nu}\langle\sum_{\nu^{\prime}}[\tilde{\mathbf{h}}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}]_{\boldsymbol{\eta}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\eta}}{\rm v}_{\nu}\rangle

(324)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\nu}\langle\sum_{\nu^{\prime}}\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2,\eta^{\prime}\eta}(\nu^{\prime},\nu)(\delta_{\nu,-1}{\rm v}+\nu{\rm v}_{+})\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle\sum_{\nu^{\prime}}\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2,\eta^{\prime}\eta}(\nu^{\prime},-1){\rm v}\rangle+\sum_{\nu}\langle\sum_{\nu^{\prime}}\nu\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2,\eta^{\prime}\eta}(\nu^{\prime},\nu){\rm v}_{+}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\Gamma}{2\hbar}\delta_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}+\sum_{\nu}\langle\sum_{\nu^{\prime}}\nu\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2,\eta^{\prime}\eta}(\nu^{\prime},\nu){\rm v}_{+}\rangle

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle-\frac{\Gamma}{2\hbar}\delta_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}+\langle{\rm K}_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}^{\sigma(\sigma)}{\rm v}_{+}\rangle\;,

where we have introduced the
2\times 2
matrix
\mathbf{K}^{\sigma(\sigma)}
of elements
{\rm K}_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}^{\sigma(\sigma)}:=\sum_{\nu}\sum_{\nu^{\prime}}\nu\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2,\eta^{\prime}\eta}(\nu^{\prime},\nu)
and where the vertex reads

{\rm v}_{\nu}=-\frac{|{\rm t}_{\alpha}(\epsilon_{k})|^{2}}{\hbar^{2}}f^{\alpha}_{\nu}(\epsilon_{k})\;.

(325)

In Eq. (
324
), we have used the splitting of the Fermi function
f_{\nu}(x)=\delta_{\nu,-1}+\nu f_{+}(x)
in the vertex. Also, we assumed that
\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2,\eta^{\prime}\eta}(\nu^{\prime},-1)
has no poles in the upper complex plane, so that the contraction with the temperature-independent vertex
{\rm v}
simply yields
-\Gamma/2\hbar
, as in the non-dressed case, see Eqs. (
140
) and (
170
).
The matrix
\tilde{\mathbf{h}}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}
has a
4\times 4
structure in the collective sojourn index
\boldsymbol{\eta}=(\nu,\eta)
induced by the third-tier bubble
\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{\sigma(\sigma)}
.

In order to avoid the inversion of a four-dimensional matrix to evaluate
\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{2}
,
we exploit the specific form of the third-tier bubbles forming
\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{\sigma(\sigma)}
in the NCA4 and use a two-step procedure.
We start by writing the matrix element of the
4\times 4
third-tier bubble as

}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{60.94984pt}{-18.23921pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{66}\lxSVG@closescope }}}

\lxSVG@closescope {}{{}}{}{{{}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \color[rgb]{0,0,1}{}\lxSVG@fillstroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 39.37 11.81 M 43.52 11.81 C 43.52 14.1 41.66 15.96 39.37 15.96 C 37.08 15.96 35.22 14.1 35.22 11.81 C 35.22 9.52 37.08 7.66 39.37 7.66 C 41.66 7.66 43.52 9.52 43.52 11.81 Z M 39.37 11.81}{} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces

{}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \color[rgb]{1,1,1}\lxSVG@fill\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 106.3 -31.5 M 106.3 -31.5 L 106.3 23.62 L 51.18 23.62 L 51.18 -31.5 Z M 51.18 23.62}{stroke:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope {}{{}}{}
{}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 106.3 -31.5 M 106.3 -31.5 L 106.3 23.62 L 51.18 23.62 L 51.18 -31.5 Z M 51.18 23.62}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 78.74 -7.87}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{}{{

{}{}}
{{}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}}{{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{}{{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{38.99573pt}{-9.7543pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{66}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}
}\end{gathered}=\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\nu^{\prime}\nu}^{A}(\eta)+\nu^{\prime}\nu\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\eta^{\prime}\eta}^{B}(\nu)\;.

(326)

Note that
\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\eta^{\prime}\eta}^{B}(\nu)
is independent of
\nu^{\prime}
and
\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\nu^{\prime}\nu}^{A}(\eta)
is independent of
\eta^{\prime}
, as shown in the above diagrams.
It is convenient to define separately the first and second contribution to
\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\boldsymbol{\eta}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\eta}}^{\sigma(\sigma)}
, in Eq. (
326
) as two
2\times 2
matrices, denoted with
A
and
B
respectively. Let the first,
\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{A}(\eta)
, be a matrix in the indexes
\nu^{\prime}\nu
with explicit dependence on the vertex index
\eta
. Specifically, the matrix elements of
\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{A}(\eta)
are given by the contraction of a propagator dressed by 4th-tier bubbles

\displaystyle\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\nu^{\prime}\nu}^{A}(\eta):=\langle\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\nu^{\prime}\nu}{\rm v}_{-\eta}\rangle\;,

(327)

where

\displaystyle\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\nu\nu}=

\displaystyle\frac{({\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\bar{\nu}\bar{\nu}})^{-1}-{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{4,\bar{\nu}\bar{\nu}}}{D^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}}\;,\qquad\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\bar{\nu}\nu}=\frac{{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{4,\bar{\nu}\nu}}{D^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}}\;,

(328)

with
{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\nu^{\prime}\nu}
given in Eq. (
231
) and with
D^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}=[({\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\nu\nu})^{-1}-{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{4,\nu\nu}][({\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\bar{\nu}\bar{\nu}})^{-1}-{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{4,\bar{\nu}\bar{\nu}}]-{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{4,\nu\bar{\nu}}{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{4,\bar{\nu}\nu}
a function of the energies, independent of the indexes
\nu^{\prime}\nu
. As shown in Appendix
J
, the bare 4th-tier bubbles
{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{4,\nu^{\prime}\nu}
, with NCA2 structure, are

\displaystyle{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{4,\nu^{\prime}\nu}=-\frac{\Gamma}{2\hbar}\delta_{\nu^{\prime}\nu}+\nu^{\prime}\nu{\rm F}^{(\sigma)}_{\nu}\qquad{\rm where}\qquad{\rm F}^{(\sigma)}_{\nu}=-\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}\Sigma_{4,\sigma\nu}^{(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}\;.

(329)

The symmetries of the fourth-tier bubble imply

\displaystyle\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\nu\nu}=

\displaystyle\frac{({\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\bar{\nu}\bar{\nu}})^{-1}+\Gamma/2\hbar}{D^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}}-\frac{{\rm F}^{(\sigma)}_{\bar{\nu}}}{D^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}}

(330)

\displaystyle\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\bar{\nu}\nu}=

\displaystyle-\frac{{\rm F}^{(\sigma)}_{\nu}}{D^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}}\;.

Thus, Eq. (
327
) yields

\displaystyle\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\nu\nu}^{A}(\eta)=

\displaystyle\Biggl<\frac{({\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\bar{\nu}\bar{\nu}})^{-1}+\Gamma/2\hbar}{D^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}}{\rm v}_{-\eta}\Biggr>-\Biggl<\frac{{\rm F}^{(\sigma)}_{\bar{\nu}}}{D^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}}{\rm v}_{-\eta}\Biggr>

(331)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle A^{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu,\eta)-\Biggl<\frac{{\rm F}^{(\sigma)}_{\bar{\nu}}}{D^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}}{\rm v}_{-\eta}\Biggr>

\displaystyle\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\bar{\nu}\nu}^{A}(\eta)=

\displaystyle-\Biggl<\frac{{\rm F}^{(\sigma)}_{\nu}}{D^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}}{\rm v}_{-\eta}\Biggr>\;.

For later purposes, let us define the matrix given by the sum over the vertex
\mathbf{S}_{3}^{A}:=\sum_{\eta}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{A}(\eta)
and its off-diagonal elements

{\rm s}_{\nu}^{A}:=\langle[{\rm F}^{(\sigma)}_{\nu}/D^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}]{\rm v}\rangle\;.

(332)

We can express
\mathbf{S}_{3}^{A}
in terms of its off-diagonal elements as

\displaystyle{\rm S}_{3,\nu\nu}^{A}:=

\displaystyle\sum_{\eta}\langle\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\nu\nu}{\rm v}_{-\eta}\rangle=\langle\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\nu\nu}{\rm v}\rangle=\sum_{\eta}A^{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu,\eta)-{\rm s}_{\bar{\nu}}^{A}=-\frac{\Gamma}{\hbar}+{\rm s}_{\nu}^{A}

(333)

\displaystyle{\rm S}_{3,\bar{\nu}\nu}^{A}:=

\displaystyle\sum_{\eta}\langle\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\bar{\nu}\nu}{\rm v}_{-\eta}\rangle=\langle\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\bar{\nu}\nu}{\rm v}\rangle=-{\rm s}_{\nu}^{A}\;,

where we used
\sum_{\eta}A^{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu,\eta)-\sum_{\nu}{\rm s}_{\nu}^{A}=\sum_{\eta}\langle(\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\nu\nu}+\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\bar{\nu}\nu}){\rm v}_{-\eta}\rangle=-\Gamma/\hbar
, assuming that the function

\displaystyle\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\nu\nu}+\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\bar{\nu}\nu}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{({\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\nu\nu})^{-1}-{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{4,\nu\nu}-{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{4,\bar{\nu}\nu}\frac{({\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\nu\nu})^{-1}-{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{4,\nu\nu}+{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{4,\nu\bar{\nu}}}{({\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,\bar{\nu}\bar{\nu}})^{-1}-{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{4,\bar{\nu}\bar{\nu}}+{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{4,\bar{\nu}\nu}}}

(334)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{{\rm i}\zeta\hbar}{\epsilon-\epsilon_{k_{1}}+\zeta\zeta_{2}[\epsilon_{k_{2}}-E_{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu)]+{\rm i}\zeta\Gamma/2-\zeta_{2}\nu\Sigma_{4,\sigma\nu}^{(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}\frac{U}{\epsilon-\epsilon_{k_{1}}+\zeta\zeta_{2}[\epsilon_{k_{2}}-E_{\bar{\sigma}}(\bar{\nu})]+{\rm i}\zeta 3\Gamma/2}}\;

is analytical in the upper complex plane.
Here, we used Eqs. (
329
)-(
330
) and (
231
), and
E_{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu)-E_{\bar{\sigma}}(\bar{\nu})=\nu U
. Note the formal similarity with the terms of the NCA2 Green’s function, Eqs. (
189
) and (
191
).
As a matrix with indexes
\nu^{\prime}\nu
,
\mathbf{S}_{3}^{A}
reads

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{3}^{A}=

\displaystyle-\frac{\Gamma}{\hbar}\begin{pmatrix}1&0\\
0&1\end{pmatrix}+\begin{pmatrix}{\rm s}_{+}^{A}&-{\rm s}_{-}^{A}\\
-{\rm s}_{+}^{A}&{\rm s}_{-}^{A}\end{pmatrix}

(335)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\Gamma}{\hbar}\mathbf{1}+\mathbf{s}^{A}\;.

Analogously, we introduce the
2\times 2
matrix
\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{B}(\nu)
in the indexes
\eta^{\prime}\eta
with explicit dependence on the vertex index
\nu
, see the second term in Eq. (
326
). The matrix element of
\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{B}(\nu)
is

\displaystyle\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\eta^{\prime}\eta}^{B}(\nu):=\langle\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{3,\eta^{\prime}\eta}{\rm v}_{-\nu}\rangle\;,

(336)

where

\displaystyle\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{3,\eta\eta}=

\displaystyle\frac{({\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{3,\bar{\eta}\bar{\eta}})^{-1}-{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{4,\bar{\eta}\bar{\eta}}}{D^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}}\;,\qquad\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{3,\bar{\eta}\eta}=\frac{{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{4,\bar{\eta}\eta}}{D^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}}\;.

(337)

The bare 4th-tier bubbles
{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{4,\eta^{\prime}\eta}
, with NCA2 structure, can be written as

\displaystyle{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{4,\eta^{\prime}\eta}=-\frac{\Gamma}{2\hbar}\delta_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}+{\rm F}^{(\bar{\sigma})}_{\eta}\qquad{\rm where}\qquad{\rm F}^{(\bar{\sigma})}_{\eta}=-\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}\Sigma_{4,\sigma\eta}^{(\sigma\sigma)}\;,

(338)

see Appendix
J
.
As above, also in this case the diagonal elements can be expressed in terms of the off-diagonal ones, which results in

\displaystyle\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\eta\eta}^{B}(\nu)=

\displaystyle\Biggl<\frac{({\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{3,\bar{\eta}\bar{\eta}})^{-1}+\Gamma/2\hbar}{D^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}}{\rm v}_{-\nu}\Biggr>-\Biggl<\frac{{\rm F}^{(\bar{\sigma})}_{\bar{\eta}}}{D^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}}{\rm v}_{-\nu}\Biggr>

(339)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle A^{\sigma}(\eta,\nu)-\Biggl<\frac{{\rm F}^{(\bar{\sigma})}_{\bar{\eta}}}{D^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}}{\rm v}_{-\nu}\Biggr>

\displaystyle\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\bar{\eta}\eta}^{B}(\nu)=

\displaystyle\Biggl<\frac{{\rm F}^{(\bar{\sigma})}_{\eta}}{D^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}}{\rm v}_{-\nu}\Biggr>\;,

with
D^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}=[({\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{3,\eta\eta})^{-1}-{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{4,\eta\eta}][({\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{3,\bar{\eta}\bar{\eta}})^{-1}-{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{4,\bar{\eta}\bar{\eta}}]-{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{4,\eta\bar{\eta}}{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{4,\bar{\eta}\eta}
.
For the matrix
\mathbf{S}_{3}^{B}:=\sum_{\nu}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{B}(\nu)
of indexes
\eta^{\prime}\eta
we get

\displaystyle{\rm S}_{3,\eta\eta}^{B}:=

\displaystyle\sum_{\nu}\langle\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{3,\eta\eta}{\rm v}_{-\nu}\rangle=\langle\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{3,\eta\eta}{\rm v}\rangle=\sum_{\eta}A^{\sigma}(\eta,\nu)-{\rm s}_{\bar{\eta}}^{B}=-\frac{\Gamma}{\hbar}+{\rm s}_{\eta}^{B}

(340)

\displaystyle{\rm S}_{3,\bar{\eta}\eta}^{B}:=

\displaystyle\sum_{\nu}\langle\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{3,\bar{\eta}\eta}{\rm v}_{-\nu}\rangle=\langle\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{3,\bar{\eta}\eta}{\rm v}\rangle={\rm s}_{\eta}^{B}\;,

where
{\rm s}_{\eta}^{B}:=\langle[{\rm F}^{(\bar{\sigma})}_{\eta}/D^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}]{\rm v}\rangle
.
Similarly as for
{\rm S}_{3,\nu^{\prime}\nu}^{A}
, we used
\sum_{\nu}A^{\sigma}(\eta,\nu)-\sum_{\eta}{\rm s}_{\eta}^{B}=\sum_{\nu}\langle(\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{3,\eta\eta}-\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{3,\bar{\eta}\eta}){\rm v}_{-\nu}\rangle=-\Gamma/\hbar
, assuming that the function

\displaystyle\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{3,\eta\eta}-\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{3,\bar{\eta}\eta}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{({\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{3,\eta\eta})^{-1}-{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{4,\eta\eta}+{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{4,\bar{\eta}\eta}\frac{({\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{3,\eta\eta})^{-1}-{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{4,\eta\eta}-{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{4,\eta\bar{\eta}}}{({\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{3,\bar{\eta}\bar{\eta}})^{-1}-{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{4,\bar{\eta}\bar{\eta}}-{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma\sigma)}_{4,\bar{\eta}\eta}}}

(341)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{{\rm i}\zeta\hbar}{\epsilon-\epsilon_{k_{1}}+\zeta\zeta_{2}[\epsilon_{k_{2}}-E_{\sigma}(\eta)]+{\rm i}\zeta\Gamma/2-\zeta_{2}\eta\Sigma_{4,\sigma\eta}^{(\sigma\sigma)}\frac{U}{\epsilon-\epsilon_{k_{1}}+\zeta\zeta_{2}[\epsilon_{k_{2}}-E_{\sigma}(\bar{\eta})]+{\rm i}\zeta 3\Gamma/2}}\;,

see Eqs. (
337
)-(
338
) and (
231
), is analytical in the upper complex plane.
In matrix form, with indexes
\eta^{\prime}\eta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{3}^{B}=

\displaystyle-\frac{\Gamma}{\hbar}\begin{pmatrix}1&0\\
0&1\end{pmatrix}+\begin{pmatrix}{\rm s}_{+}^{B}&{\rm s}_{-}^{B}\\
{\rm s}_{+}^{B}&{\rm s}_{-}^{B}\end{pmatrix}

(342)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\Gamma}{\hbar}\mathbf{1}+\mathbf{s}^{B}\;.

The dressed bubble
\tilde{\mathbf{B}}^{\sigma(\sigma)}
, with
2\times 2
structure in
\eta^{\prime}\eta
, see Eq. (
324
), is then obtained as the contraction

\displaystyle\tilde{\rm B}_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}^{\sigma(\sigma)}=

\displaystyle\begin{gathered}\resizebox{}{3169949}{

{}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \color[rgb]{1,1,1}\lxSVG@fill\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 188.98 -31.5 M 188.98 -31.5 L 188.98 15.75 L 224.41 15.75 L 224.41 -31.5 Z M 224.41 15.75}{stroke:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope {}{{}}{}
{}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 188.98 -31.5 M 188.98 -31.5 L 188.98 15.75 L 224.41 15.75 L 224.41 -31.5 Z M 224.41 15.75}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
{}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 204.72 -7.87}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{}{{
{}{}}}{
{}{}}
{{}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}}{{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{}{{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{140.08424pt}{-9.53046pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{66}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}
}\end{gathered}+\dots

(344)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\delta_{\eta^{\prime},\eta}+{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{A}(\eta){\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}+{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\sum_{\eta^{\prime\prime}}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{A}(\eta^{\prime\prime}){\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{A}(\eta){\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}+\dots

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\delta_{\eta^{\prime},\eta}+{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{A}(\eta){\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}+{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\mathbf{S}^{A}_{3}{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{A}(\eta){\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}+\dots

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\delta_{\eta^{\prime},\eta}+\frac{{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}}{\mathbf{1}[{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}]^{-1}-\mathbf{S}^{A}_{3}}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{A}(\eta)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\delta_{\eta^{\prime},\eta}+\tilde{\mathbf{C}}^{A}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{A}(\eta)\;,

where
\mathbf{S}^{A}_{3}
has been introduced above and

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathbf{C}}^{A}:=\frac{{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}}{\mathbf{1}[{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}]^{-1}-\mathbf{S}^{A}_{3}}\;.

(345)

Inversion of the matrix in the denominator of
\tilde{\mathbf{C}}^{A}
then yields

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathbf{C}}^{A}=

\displaystyle\frac{{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}}{([{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}]^{-1}+\Gamma/\hbar)([{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}]^{-1}+\Gamma/\hbar-{\rm s}_{+}^{A}-{\rm s}_{-}^{A})}\begin{pmatrix}[{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}]^{-1}+\Gamma/\hbar-{\rm s}_{-}^{A}&\;-{\rm s}_{-}^{A}\\
-{\rm s}_{+}^{A}&\;[{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}]^{-1}+\Gamma/\hbar-{\rm s}_{+}^{A}\end{pmatrix}

(346)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\rm{\displaystyle h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\left[\mathbf{1}+\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{A}\begin{pmatrix}{\rm s}_{+}^{A}&-{\rm s}_{-}^{A}\\
-{\rm s}_{+}^{A}&{\rm s}_{-}^{A}\end{pmatrix}\right]\;,

where

\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}:=\frac{1}{[{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}]^{-1}+\Gamma/\hbar}\;,\qquad\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{A}:=\frac{1}{[{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}]^{-1}+\Gamma/\hbar-{\rm s}^{A}}\;,\qquad{\rm and}\qquad{\rm s}^{A}:=\sum_{\nu}{\rm s}_{\nu}^{A}=-\frac{\Gamma}{\hbar}\langle[D^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}]^{-1}{\rm v}\rangle\;.

(347)

Now that we have a closed form for
\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{2}^{A}(\eta^{\prime},\eta)
, let us switch to representing every quantity as a matrix in
\eta^{\prime}\eta
while making explicit the dependencies on
\nu^{\prime}
and
\nu
. This is natural for
\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{B}(\nu)
. On the other hand, as an intermediate step, we express
\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{2}^{A}(\eta^{\prime},\eta)
, Eq. (
344
), as the matrix element

\displaystyle\tilde{{\rm h}}_{2,\eta^{\prime}\eta}^{A}(\nu^{\prime},\nu)={\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\delta_{\eta^{\prime},\eta}\delta_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}+\sum_{\nu^{\prime\prime}}\tilde{\rm C}^{A}(\nu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime\prime})\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\eta^{\prime}\eta}^{A}(\nu^{\prime\prime},\nu)\;,

(348)

where the product
\nu^{\prime\prime}\nu
is associated to the overlap of three spin-
\sigma
fermion lines in
\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\eta\eta}^{A}(\nu^{\prime},\nu)
, see Eq. (
326
). Due to the sole dependency on the first-sojourn index
\eta
, the following property holds

\displaystyle\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\eta\eta}^{A}(\nu^{\prime},\nu)=\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\bar{\eta}\eta}^{A}(\nu^{\prime},\nu)\;.

(349)

Moreover, from Eqs. (
331
) and (
339
),
\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\eta^{\prime}\eta}^{A}(\nu^{\prime},\nu)
and
\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\eta^{\prime}\eta}^{B}(\nu)
have the symmetries

\displaystyle\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\eta^{\prime}\eta}^{A}(\nu,\nu)=

\displaystyle A^{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu,\eta)+\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\eta^{\prime}\eta}^{A}(\nu,\bar{\nu})\;,

(350)

\displaystyle\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\eta\eta}^{B}(\nu)=

\displaystyle A^{\sigma}(\eta,\nu)-\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\eta\bar{\eta}}^{B}(\nu)\;.

From Eqs. (
346
) and (
347
), the function
\tilde{\rm C}^{A}(\nu^{\prime},\nu)
, which does not depend on
\eta
, reads

\displaystyle\tilde{\rm C}^{A}(\nu^{\prime},\nu)=

\displaystyle\rm{\displaystyle h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\left(\delta_{\nu^{\prime}\nu}+\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{A}\nu^{\prime}\nu\;{\rm s}_{\nu}^{A}\right)\;.

(351)

Therefore, as a matrix in
(\eta^{\prime},\eta)

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{2}^{A}(\nu^{\prime},\nu)=

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\delta_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}+\sum_{\nu^{\prime\prime}}\tilde{\rm C}^{A}(\nu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime\prime})\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{A}(\nu^{\prime\prime},\nu)\;.

(352)

Finally, let us introduce the matrices

\displaystyle\sum_{\nu}\nu\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{2}^{A}(\nu^{\prime},\nu):=

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\sum_{\nu}\nu\delta_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}+\mathbf{K}^{A}(\nu^{\prime})\;,

(353)

\displaystyle\mathbf{K}^{A}(\nu^{\prime}):=

\displaystyle\sum_{\nu}\nu\sum_{\nu^{\prime\prime}}\tilde{\rm C}^{A}(\nu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime\prime})\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{A}(\nu^{\prime\prime},\nu)\;,

\displaystyle\mathbf{K}^{A}:=

\displaystyle\sum_{\nu^{\prime}}\mathbf{K}^{A}(\nu^{\prime})\;.

Note that the matrices
\mathbf{K}^{A}(\nu)
and
\mathbf{K}^{A}
inherit the symmetry (
349
).

We are now in the position to iterate the third-tier bubbles
\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{B}
as outlined in Eq. (
343
). Accounting for the factors
\nu
and
\nu^{\prime}
associated to overlap of three spin-
\sigma
fermion lines in
\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{B}
, the second term of Eq. (
324
) yields the matrix in the indexes
\eta^{\prime}\eta

\displaystyle\mathbf{K}^{\sigma(\sigma)}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\nu^{\prime}}\sum_{\nu}\nu\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{2}^{\sigma(\sigma)}(\nu^{\prime},\nu)

(354)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\nu^{\prime}}\sum_{\nu}\nu\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{2}^{A}(\nu^{\prime},\nu)+\sum_{\nu^{\prime}}\sum_{\nu}\nu\sum_{\nu^{\prime\prime\prime}}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{2}^{A}(\nu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime\prime\prime})\nu^{\prime\prime\prime}\sum_{\nu^{\prime\prime}}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{B}(\nu^{\prime\prime})\nu^{\prime\prime}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{2}^{A}(\nu^{\prime\prime},\nu)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\nu^{\prime}}\sum_{\nu}\nu\sum_{\nu^{v}}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{2}^{A}(\nu^{\prime},\nu^{v})\nu^{v}\sum_{\nu^{iv}}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{B}(\nu^{iv})\nu^{iv}\sum_{\nu^{\prime\prime\prime}}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{2}^{A}(\nu^{iv},\nu^{\prime\prime\prime})\nu^{\prime\prime\prime}\sum_{\nu^{\prime\prime}}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{B}(\nu^{\prime\prime})\nu^{\prime\prime}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{2}^{A}(\nu^{\prime\prime},\nu)+\dots

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{K}^{A}+\mathbf{K}^{A}\sum_{\nu^{\prime\prime}}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{B}(\nu^{\prime\prime})\nu^{\prime\prime}\sum_{\nu}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{2}^{A}(\nu^{\prime\prime},\nu)\nu

\displaystyle+\mathbf{K}^{A}\sum_{\nu^{iv}}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{B}(\nu^{iv})\nu^{iv}\sum_{\nu^{\prime\prime\prime}}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{2}^{A}(\nu^{iv},\nu^{\prime\prime\prime})\nu^{\prime\prime\prime}\sum_{\nu^{\prime\prime}}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{B}(\nu^{\prime\prime})\nu^{\prime\prime}\sum_{\nu}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{2}^{A}(\nu^{\prime\prime},\nu)\nu+\dots

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{K}^{A}\frac{\mathbf{1}}{\mathbf{1}-\sum_{\nu^{\prime}}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{B}(\nu^{\prime})\nu^{\prime}\sum_{\nu}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{2}^{A}(\nu^{\prime},\nu)\nu}

\displaystyle[{\rm Eq.}~\eqref{KA}]\quad=

\displaystyle\mathbf{K}^{A}\frac{\mathbf{1}}{\mathbf{1}-{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\mathbf{S}_{3}^{B}-\sum_{\nu}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{B}(\nu)\nu\mathbf{K}^{A}(\nu)}

\displaystyle[{\rm Eq.}~\eqref{S3B}]\quad=

\displaystyle\mathbf{K}^{A}\frac{\mathbf{1}}{\mathbf{1}(1+{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\Gamma/\hbar)-{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\mathbf{s}^{B}-\sum_{\nu}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{B}(\nu)\nu\mathbf{K}^{A}(\nu)}\;.

From the properties of the third-tier bubbles, Eqs. (
331
), (
339
), (
349
)-(
350
) and the definitions of
\mathbf{K}^{A}
and
\mathbf{K}^{A}(\nu)
, Eq. (
353
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{K}^{A}_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}\tilde{\rm C}^{A}(\nu^{\prime},\nu)\nu A^{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu,\eta)

(355)

\displaystyle\left[\sum_{\nu}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{B}(\nu)\nu\mathbf{K}^{A}(\nu)\right]_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}\nu^{\prime}\nu\;\tilde{\rm C}^{A}(\nu^{\prime},\nu)A^{\sigma}(\eta^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})A^{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu,\eta)\;.

After some lengthy manipulations one obtains the following results

\displaystyle\mathbf{K}^{A}_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}=

\displaystyle\eta\;\Delta A^{\bar{\sigma}}_{+}\;{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\;,

(356)

\displaystyle\sum_{\nu}\tilde{\mathbf{B}}_{3}^{B}(\nu)\nu\mathbf{K}^{A}(\nu)=

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{1}\Delta A^{\sigma}_{+}\Delta A^{\bar{\sigma}}_{+}+\mathbf{P}\right)\;{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\tilde{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}\;,

\displaystyle{\rm where}\qquad\qquad\quad\Delta A^{\sigma/\bar{\sigma}}_{+}=

\displaystyle A^{\sigma/\bar{\sigma}}(+,+)-A^{\sigma/\bar{\sigma}}(-,+)\;,

with
\mathbf{P}_{\eta\eta}=\mathbf{P}_{\bar{\eta}\eta}
. This latter property, along with
\mathbf{s}^{B}_{\eta\eta}=\mathbf{s}^{B}_{\bar{\eta}\eta}
, yields, upon inverting the matrix
\mathbf{K}
, the key result

\displaystyle{\rm K}_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}^{\sigma(\sigma)}=

\displaystyle\eta\frac{\Delta A^{\bar{\sigma}}_{+}}{([{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}]^{-1}+\Gamma/\hbar)^{2}-\Delta A^{\sigma}_{+}\Delta A^{\bar{\sigma}}_{+}}\;.

(357)

Note that the above expression implies for the retarded self-energy
\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma\eta}^{(\sigma)}(\epsilon)=\;{\rm i}\hbar\tilde{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{\bar{\eta}\eta}(\kappa)|_{\zeta=+1}
the property

\sum_{\eta}\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma\eta}^{(\sigma)}(\epsilon)=0\;.

(358)

The explicit expression for
\Delta A^{\bar{\sigma}}_{+}=A^{\bar{\sigma}}(+,+)-A^{\bar{\sigma}}(-,+)
is found from the definition of
A^{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu,\eta)
, Eq. (
331
), adding
(\Gamma/\hbar)/D^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}
to both the contracted functions in the difference, and using Eq. (
334
). The result is

\displaystyle\Delta A^{\bar{\sigma}}_{+}=

\displaystyle\Biggl<\left[\frac{({\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,--})^{-1}+\Gamma/2\hbar}{D^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}}-\frac{({\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}_{3,++})^{-1}+\Gamma/2\hbar}{D^{\sigma(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}}\right]{\rm v}_{-}\Biggr>

(359)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Biggl<\frac{{\rm i}\zeta\hbar}{\epsilon-\epsilon_{k_{1}}+\zeta\zeta_{2}[\epsilon_{k_{2}}-E_{\bar{\sigma}}(+)]+{\rm i}\zeta\Gamma/2-\zeta_{2}\Sigma_{4,\sigma+}^{(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}\frac{U}{\epsilon-\epsilon_{k_{1}}+\zeta\zeta_{2}[\epsilon_{k_{2}}-E_{\bar{\sigma}}(-)]+{\rm i}\zeta 3\Gamma/2}}{\rm v}_{-}\Biggr>

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\Biggl<\frac{{\rm i}\zeta\hbar}{\epsilon-\epsilon_{k_{1}}+\zeta\zeta_{2}[\epsilon_{k_{2}}-E_{\bar{\sigma}}(-)]+{\rm i}\zeta\Gamma/2+\zeta_{2}\Sigma_{4,\sigma-}^{(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}\frac{U}{\epsilon-\epsilon_{k_{1}}+\zeta\zeta_{2}[\epsilon_{k_{2}}-E_{\bar{\sigma}}(+)]+{\rm i}\zeta 3\Gamma/2}}{\rm v}_{-}\Biggr>

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\nu}\nu\Biggl<\frac{{\rm i}\zeta_{2}\hbar}{\epsilon_{k_{2}}-E_{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu)+\zeta\zeta_{2}(\epsilon-\epsilon_{k_{1}})+{\rm i}\zeta_{2}\Gamma/2-\nu\zeta\Sigma_{4,\sigma\nu}^{(\sigma\bar{\sigma})}\frac{U}{\epsilon_{k_{2}}-E_{\bar{\sigma}}(\bar{\nu})+\zeta\zeta_{2}(\epsilon-\epsilon_{k_{1}})+{\rm i}\zeta_{2}3\Gamma/2}}{\rm v}_{-}\Biggr>\;,

where the contraction involves the innermost fermion line indexed with
\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{2}
and where we used
\zeta^{2}=1
. Here,
E_{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu)=\epsilon_{\bar{\sigma}}+(1+\nu)U/2
. A similar result holds for
\Delta A^{\sigma}_{+}
.
Note that this expression displays the same structure as the one for the Green’s function, Eq. (
236
); this makes evident that the renormalization of the dot energies
E_{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu)
(
E_{\sigma}(\eta)
) occurs also at the level of the self-energy and in principle at all (even) levels of the hierarchy.

Appendix L
Evaluation of the dressed third-tier bubbles in the simplified NCA4

A simplification of the NCA4 is obtained by setting to zero the nontrivial parts of the 4th-tier bubbles, namely setting

\Sigma_{4,\nu}^{(\sigma)}
in Eq. (
359
), or equivalently
{\rm F}_{\nu}^{(\sigma)}={\rm F}_{\eta}^{(\bar{\sigma})}=0
in Eqs. (
329
) and (
338
).
As a result, the definition in Eqs. (
331
) yields

\displaystyle A^{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu,\eta)

\displaystyle\simeq\begin{gathered}\resizebox{}{2237610}{

. Here, the vertex

is defined in Eq. (

), the propagator

is given in Eq. (

), and the width

(361)

Using Eq. (
315
) to solve the integral we obtain

\displaystyle A^{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu,\eta)

\displaystyle\simeq\frac{\rm i}{\hbar}\sum_{\alpha}\frac{\Gamma_{\alpha}}{2\pi}\Bigg\{\eta\Bigg[{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\zeta(\epsilon_{1}-\epsilon)+E_{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu)-{\rm i}\Gamma/2-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\zeta(\epsilon-\epsilon_{1})+E_{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu)-{\rm i}\Gamma/2-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg]

(362)

\displaystyle+{\rm i}\Bigg[\pi+\eta{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\zeta(\epsilon_{1}-\epsilon)+E_{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu)-{\rm i}\Gamma/2-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)+\eta{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\zeta(\epsilon-\epsilon_{1})+E_{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu)-{\rm i}\Gamma/2-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg]\Bigg\}\;,

where
\Gamma_{\alpha}=2\pi\varrho_{\alpha}|{\rm t}_{\alpha}|^{2}
and
\sum_{\alpha}\Gamma_{\alpha}=\Gamma
.
As a further approximation, we fix the argument
\epsilon_{1}=\epsilon
, which yields

\displaystyle A^{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu,\eta)\simeq-\sum_{\alpha}\frac{\Gamma_{\alpha}}{\hbar}\Bigg[\frac{1}{2}+\eta\frac{1}{\pi}{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma/2}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}+{\rm i}\frac{E_{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu)-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg]\;.

(363)

A similar calculation for
A^{\sigma}(\eta,\nu)
, defined in Eq. (
339
), gives

\displaystyle A^{\sigma}(\eta,\nu)\simeq-\sum_{\alpha}\frac{\Gamma_{\alpha}}{\hbar}\Bigg[\frac{1}{2}+\nu\frac{1}{\pi}{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma/2}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}+{\rm i}\frac{E_{\sigma}(\eta)-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg]\;.

(364)

The 3rd-tier bubble of type
\sigma(\bar{\sigma})
does not have a matrix structure and is given by the contraction of the propagators in Eq. (
232
), dressed by the simplified fourth-tier bubble
{\rm B}_{4}=-\Gamma/2
, with the vertex
{\rm v}_{{\rm x}}=-|{\rm t}_{\alpha}(\epsilon_{k})|^{2}f^{\alpha}_{{\rm x}}(\epsilon_{k})/\hbar^{2}
, where
\rm x
stands for
\eta
or
\nu
. It is the composite bubble

{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \color[rgb]{1,1,1}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
