\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}
}\end{gathered}

(365)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\hbar\sum_{\kappa_{2}}\left[\sum_{\nu}\frac{{\rm v}_{\nu}^{\alpha_{2}}(\epsilon_{k_{2}})\delta_{\zeta_{2},-\zeta}}{\Gamma/2-{\rm i}\zeta(\epsilon_{k}-\epsilon_{k_{2}})-{\rm i}\zeta_{1}[\epsilon_{k_{1}}-E_{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu)]}+\sum_{\eta}\frac{{\rm v}_{\eta}^{\alpha_{2}}(k_{2})\delta_{\zeta_{2},-\zeta_{1}}}{\Gamma/2-{\rm i}\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{k_{1}}-\epsilon_{k_{2}})-{\rm i}\zeta[\epsilon_{k}-E_{\sigma}(\eta)]}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{{\rm i}\zeta}{\hbar}\sum_{\alpha_{2}}\varrho_{\alpha_{2}}|{\rm t}_{\alpha_{2}}|^{2}\sum_{\nu}\int_{-W}^{W}d\epsilon_{2}\;\frac{f^{\alpha_{2}}_{\nu}(\epsilon_{2})}{\epsilon_{2}-\epsilon+\zeta\zeta_{1}[E_{\bar{\sigma}}(\nu)-\epsilon_{1}]-{\rm i}\zeta\Gamma/2}

\displaystyle+\frac{{\rm i}\zeta_{1}}{\hbar}\sum_{\alpha_{2}}\varrho_{\alpha_{2}}|{\rm t}_{\alpha_{2}}|^{2}\sum_{\eta}\int_{-W}^{W}d\epsilon_{2}\;\frac{f^{\alpha_{2}}_{\eta}(\epsilon_{2})}{\epsilon_{2}-\epsilon_{1}+\zeta\zeta_{1}[E_{\sigma}(\eta)-\epsilon]-{\rm i}\zeta_{1}\Gamma/2}\;,

where
E_{\sigma}(\eta)=\epsilon_{\sigma}+(1+\eta)U/2
. From Eq. (
315
) we obtain

\displaystyle\tilde{\rm B}_{3}^{\sigma(\bar{\sigma})}\simeq\frac{{\rm i}}{\hbar}\sum_{\alpha}\frac{\Gamma_{\alpha}}{2\pi}\Bigg\{

\displaystyle\zeta{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon-\zeta\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\bar{\sigma}}+U-\epsilon_{1})-{\rm i}\Gamma/2-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-\zeta{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon-\zeta\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\bar{\sigma}}-\epsilon_{1})-{\rm i}\Gamma/2-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)

(366)

\displaystyle+\zeta_{1}

\displaystyle\rm{\displaystyle Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon_{1}-\zeta\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\sigma}+U-\epsilon)-{\rm i}\Gamma/2-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-\zeta_{1}{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon_{1}-\zeta\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\sigma}-\epsilon)-{\rm i}\Gamma/2-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)

\displaystyle+{\rm i}\Bigg[2\pi-

\displaystyle\rm{\displaystyle Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon-\zeta\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\bar{\sigma}}+U-\epsilon_{1})-{\rm i}\Gamma/2-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)+{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon-\zeta\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\bar{\sigma}}-\epsilon_{1})-{\rm i}\Gamma/2-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\rm{\displaystyle Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon_{1}-\zeta\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\sigma}+U-\epsilon)-{\rm i}\Gamma/2-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)+{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon_{1}-\zeta\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\sigma}-\epsilon)-{\rm i}\Gamma/2-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg]\Bigg\}\;.

Taking
\epsilon=\epsilon_{1}=\mu_{\alpha}
,
the retarded (
\zeta=+1
) third-tier self-energies
\tilde{\Sigma}_{3\sigma,\zeta_{1}}^{(\bar{\sigma})}:={\rm i}\hbar\tilde{\rm B}_{3,\zeta_{1}}^{\sigma(\bar{\sigma})}|_{\zeta=+1}
read

\displaystyle\tilde{\Sigma}_{3\sigma,\zeta_{1}}^{(\bar{\sigma})}\simeq-\sum_{\alpha}\frac{\Gamma_{\alpha}}{2\pi}\Bigg\{

\displaystyle\rm{\displaystyle Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}-{\rm i}\frac{\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\bar{\sigma}}+U-\mu_{\alpha})+{\rm i}\Gamma/2}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}-{\rm i}\frac{\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\bar{\sigma}}-\mu_{\alpha})+{\rm i}\Gamma/2}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)

(367)

\displaystyle+\zeta_{1}

\displaystyle\rm{\displaystyle Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}-{\rm i}\frac{\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\sigma}+U-\mu_{\alpha})+{\rm i}\Gamma/2}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-\zeta_{1}{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}-{\rm i}\frac{\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\sigma}-\mu_{\alpha})+{\rm i}\Gamma/2}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)

\displaystyle+{\rm i}\Bigg[2\pi-

\displaystyle\rm{\displaystyle Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}-{\rm i}\frac{\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\bar{\sigma}}+U-\mu_{\alpha})+{\rm i}\Gamma/2}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)+{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}-{\rm i}\frac{\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\bar{\sigma}}-\mu_{\alpha})+{\rm i}\Gamma/2}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\rm{\displaystyle Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}-{\rm i}\frac{\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\sigma}+U-\mu_{\alpha})+{\rm i}\Gamma/2}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)+{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}-{\rm i}\frac{\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\sigma}-\mu_{\alpha})+{\rm i}\Gamma/2}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg]\Bigg\}\;.

Consider the degenerate case,
\epsilon_{\uparrow}=\epsilon_{\downarrow}=\epsilon_{0}
, at equilibrium
\mu_{\alpha}=\mu
. At the particle-hole symmetry point
\mu-\epsilon_{0}=U/2
, Eq. (
367
) simplifies to

\displaystyle\tilde{\Sigma}_{3\sigma,\pm}^{(\bar{\sigma})}\simeq-{\rm i}\Gamma\Bigg[1\pm\frac{2}{\pi}{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma/2}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}+{\rm i}\frac{U/2}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg]\;.

(368)

Appendix M
Evaluation of the dressed bubble
\tilde{\mathbf{B}}^{\sigma(\sigma)}
within the simplified NCA4

Within the simplified NCA4, we can give an explicit expression for the functions
\Delta A_{+}^{\bar{\sigma}/\sigma}
appearing in
{\rm K}_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}^{\sigma(\sigma)}
, Eq. (
357
).
Using the simplified third-tier bubbles, Eqs. (
363
)-(
364
), we find

\displaystyle\Delta A_{+}^{\bar{\sigma}}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\alpha}\frac{\Gamma_{\alpha}}{\hbar}\Bigg[\frac{1}{\pi}{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma/2}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon_{\bar{\sigma}}+U-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-\frac{1}{\pi}{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma/2}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon_{\bar{\sigma}}-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg]\;,

(369)

\displaystyle\Delta A_{+}^{\sigma}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\alpha}\frac{\Gamma_{\alpha}}{\hbar}\Bigg[\frac{1}{\pi}{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma/2}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon_{\sigma}+U-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-\frac{1}{\pi}{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma/2}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon_{\sigma}-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg]\;.

We can write
{\rm K}_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}^{\sigma(\sigma)}
as

\displaystyle{\rm K}_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}^{\sigma(\sigma)}=

\displaystyle-\eta\frac{\Delta A_{+}^{\bar{\sigma}}}{2(\Delta A_{+}^{\sigma}\Delta A_{+}^{\bar{\sigma}})^{1/2}}\left[\frac{1}{[{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}]^{-1}+\Gamma/\hbar+(\Delta A_{+}^{\sigma}\Delta A_{+}^{\bar{\sigma}})^{1/2}}-\frac{1}{[{\rm h}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{2}]^{-1}+\Gamma/\hbar-(\Delta A_{+}^{\sigma}\Delta A_{+}^{\bar{\sigma}})^{1/2}}\right]\;.

(370)

Since the above expression is independent of
\eta^{\prime}
, the bubble
\tilde{\mathbf{B}}^{\sigma(\sigma)}=-(\Gamma/2\hbar)\mathbf{1}+\langle\mathbf{K}^{\sigma(\sigma)}{\rm v}_{+}\rangle
has the property, see Eq. (
324
),

\tilde{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}=-\frac{\Gamma}{2\hbar}\delta_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}+\tilde{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{\bar{\eta}\eta}\;.

(371)

Noting that
\Delta A_{+}^{\bar{\sigma}/\sigma}=-|\Delta A_{+}^{\bar{\sigma}/\sigma}|
, the off-diagonal elements are

\displaystyle\tilde{\rm B}_{\bar{\eta}\eta}^{\sigma(\sigma)}

\displaystyle=\langle{\rm K}_{\bar{\eta}\eta}^{\sigma(\sigma)}{\rm v}_{+}\rangle

(372)

\displaystyle=\frac{{\rm i}\zeta}{\hbar}\frac{\eta}{2}\sqrt{\frac{|\Delta A_{+}^{\bar{\sigma}}|}{|\Delta A_{+}^{\sigma}|}}\sum_{\alpha_{1}}\varrho_{\alpha_{1}}|{\rm t}_{\alpha_{1}}|^{2}\int_{-W}^{W}d\epsilon_{1}\left[\frac{f^{\alpha_{1}}_{+}(\epsilon_{1})}{\epsilon_{1}-\epsilon-{\rm i}\zeta\Gamma_{+}}-\frac{f^{\alpha_{1}}_{+}(\epsilon_{1})}{\epsilon_{1}-\epsilon-{\rm i}\zeta\Gamma_{-}}\right]\;,

where
\Gamma_{\pm}=\Gamma\pm\hbar(\Delta A_{+}^{\sigma}\Delta A_{+}^{\bar{\sigma}})^{1/2}
.
Using Eq. (
314
) to perform the integrations we obtain for the corresponding retarded self-energies

\displaystyle\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma\eta}^{(\sigma)}(\epsilon)=

\displaystyle\rm{\displaystyle i}\hbar\tilde{\rm B}^{\sigma(\sigma)}_{\bar{\eta}\eta}(\kappa)|_{\zeta=+1}

(373)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\eta}{2}\sqrt{\frac{|\Delta A_{+}^{\bar{\sigma}}|}{|\Delta A_{+}^{\sigma}|}}\sum_{\alpha}\frac{\Gamma_{\alpha}}{2\pi}\Bigg\{{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma_{+}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma_{-}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)

\displaystyle-{\rm i}\Bigg[{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma_{+}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma_{-}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg]\Bigg\}\;.

Note that these self-energies maintain the property of the full NCA4 result in Eq. (
358
).
At equilibrium,
\mu_{L}=\mu_{R}=\mu
, and in the degenerate case,
\epsilon_{\uparrow}=\epsilon_{\downarrow}=\epsilon_{0}
,

\displaystyle\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma\eta}^{(\sigma)}(\epsilon)\simeq

\displaystyle-\frac{\eta}{2}\frac{\Gamma}{2\pi}\Bigg\{\psi^{*}\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma_{+}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-\psi^{*}\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma_{-}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg\}\;.

(374)

The self-energies are well-behaved in the limit
T\rightarrow 0
, though they do not yield the correct unitary limit for the conductance. Moreover, the correct behavior for the exponent of the expression for the Kondo temperature is not captured by this scheme. Indeed, for
\mu-\epsilon_{0},U-\mu+\epsilon_{0}\gg\Gamma
, from Eq. (
369
)
\Delta A_{+}^{\bar{\sigma}}=\Delta A_{+}^{\sigma}\sim-\Gamma/\hbar
and the dressed self-energy
\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma-}^{(\sigma)}(\mu)
is approximated, at low temperature, by

\displaystyle\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma,-}^{(\sigma)}(\mu)\simeq

\displaystyle\frac{\Gamma}{4\pi}\left[{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{2\Gamma}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)\right]

(375)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\frac{\Gamma}{4\pi}{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{2\Gamma}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\;.

As shown in the main text, the prefactor yields an incorrect exponent in the Kondo temperature.

At the particle-hole symmetry point
\mu-\epsilon_{0}=U/2
,

\displaystyle\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma\eta}^{(\sigma)}(\mu)=-\eta\frac{\Gamma}{4\pi}\Bigg\{{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma_{+}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma_{-}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg\}\;,

(376)

where, since
\Delta A_{+}^{\bar{\sigma}}=\Delta A_{+}^{\sigma}=\Delta A_{+}
, the arguments of the digamma function read

\Gamma_{\pm}=\Gamma\pm\hbar\Delta A_{+}=\Gamma\Bigg[1\pm\frac{2}{\pi}{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma/2}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}+{\rm i}\frac{U/2}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg]\;.

Note that, according to Eq. (
368
),

\tilde{\Sigma}_{3\sigma,\pm}^{(\bar{\sigma})}=-{\rm i}\Gamma_{\pm}\;.

(377)

Appendix N
Evaluation of the dressed bubble
\tilde{\mathbf{B}}^{\sigma(\bar{\sigma})}

In the NCA, the dressed bubble
\tilde{\mathbf{B}}^{\sigma(\bar{\sigma})}
is given by iteratively inserting the composite 3rd-level bubble
\tilde{\rm B}_{3}^{\sigma(\bar{\sigma})}
in Eq (
224
) which results in the geometrical series

\displaystyle\tilde{\rm B}^{\sigma(\bar{\sigma})}_{\eta^{\prime}\eta}=

\displaystyle\Biggl<\frac{1}{[{\rm h}_{2}^{\sigma(\bar{\sigma})}]^{-1}-\tilde{\rm B}_{3}^{\sigma(\bar{\sigma})}}{\rm v}_{-\eta}\Biggr>\;,

(378)

where the bare propagator
{\rm h}_{2}^{\sigma(\bar{\sigma})}
is
given by

{\rm h}_{2}^{\sigma(\bar{\sigma})}={\rm i}\hbar\frac{1}{\zeta(\epsilon_{k}-E_{\sigma})+\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{k_{1}}-E_{\bar{\sigma}})+{\rm i}0^{+}}

(379)

and
E_{\sigma}=\epsilon_{\sigma}-U/2
.
From Eq. (
378
), the retarded (
\zeta=+1
), dressed self-energy of type
(\bar{\sigma})
reads

\displaystyle\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma,\eta}^{(\bar{\sigma})}(\epsilon)\equiv

\displaystyle\rm{\displaystyle i}\zeta\hbar\tilde{\rm B}^{\sigma(\bar{\sigma})}_{\bar{\eta}\eta}(\kappa)|_{\zeta=+1}

(380)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\rm{\displaystyle i}\zeta\hbar^{2}\sum_{\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{1}}\frac{-(|{\rm t}_{\alpha_{1}}(\epsilon_{k_{1}})|^{2}/\hbar^{2})f^{\alpha_{1}}_{-\eta}(\epsilon_{k_{1}})}{-{\rm i}\zeta(\epsilon_{k}-\epsilon_{\sigma}-U/2\hbar)-{\rm i}\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{k_{1}}-\epsilon_{\bar{\sigma}}-U/2\hbar)-\hbar\tilde{\rm B}_{3}^{\sigma(\bar{\sigma})}|_{\zeta=+1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}}\varrho_{\alpha_{1}}|{\rm t}_{\alpha_{1}}|^{2}\sum_{\zeta_{1}}\zeta_{1}\int_{-W}^{W}d\epsilon_{1}\;\frac{f^{\alpha_{1}}_{-\eta}(\epsilon_{1})}{\epsilon_{1}-[\epsilon_{\bar{\sigma}}+\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\sigma}-\epsilon)+\delta_{\zeta_{1},+1}U]-{\rm i}\zeta_{1}\hbar\tilde{\rm B}_{3}^{\sigma(\bar{\sigma})}|_{\zeta=+1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}}\varrho_{\alpha_{1}}|{\rm t}_{\alpha_{1}}|^{2}\sum_{\zeta_{1}}\zeta_{1}\int_{-W}^{W}d\epsilon_{1}\;\frac{f^{\alpha_{1}}_{-\eta}(\epsilon_{1})}{\epsilon_{1}-[\epsilon_{\bar{\sigma}}+\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\sigma}-\epsilon)+\delta_{\zeta_{1},+1}U]-\zeta_{1}\tilde{\Sigma}_{3\sigma,\zeta_{1}}^{(\bar{\sigma})}}\;.

By summing over
\eta
and applying Eq. (
310
), one finds in the NCA (and in the lower-tier schemes)

\displaystyle\sum_{\eta}\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma\eta}^{(\bar{\sigma})}(\epsilon)=-{\rm i}\Gamma\;.

(381)

Within the NCA4, and further assuming the
3
rd-tier self-energies to be energy-independent, i.e. given by Eq. (
367
), the integral in Eq. (
380
) is readily solved as

\displaystyle\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma\eta}^{(\bar{\sigma})}(\epsilon)

\displaystyle\simeq\sum_{\alpha}\varrho_{\alpha}|{\rm t}_{\alpha}|^{2}\sum_{\zeta_{1}}\zeta_{1}\int_{-W}^{W}d\epsilon_{1}\;\frac{f^{\alpha}_{-\eta}(\epsilon_{1})}{\epsilon_{1}-[\epsilon_{\bar{\sigma}}+\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\sigma}-\epsilon)+\delta_{\zeta_{1},+1}U+\zeta_{1}{\rm Re}\;\tilde{\Sigma}_{3\sigma,\zeta_{1}}^{(\bar{\sigma})}]-{\rm i}\zeta_{1}{\rm Im}\;\tilde{\Sigma}_{3\sigma,\zeta_{1}}^{(\bar{\sigma})}}

(382)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\alpha}\varrho_{\alpha}|{\rm t}_{\alpha}|^{2}\sum_{\zeta_{1}}\zeta_{1}\int_{-W}^{W}d\epsilon_{1}\;\frac{f^{\alpha}_{-\eta}(\epsilon_{1})}{\epsilon_{1}-[\epsilon_{\bar{\sigma}}+\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\sigma}-\epsilon)+\delta_{\zeta_{1},+1}U+\zeta_{1}{\rm Re}\;\tilde{\Sigma}_{3\sigma,\zeta_{1}}^{(\bar{\sigma})}]+{\rm i}\zeta_{1}|{\rm Im}\;\tilde{\Sigma}_{3\sigma,\zeta_{1}}^{(\bar{\sigma})}|}

\displaystyle=-\eta\sum_{\alpha}\frac{\Gamma_{\alpha}}{2\pi}\left[\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{+1}-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-\psi^{*}\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{-1}-\mu_{\alpha}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\right]-{\rm i}\frac{\Gamma}{2}\;,

where, in the second line, we used explicitly the fact that the imaginary part of
\tilde{\Sigma}_{3\sigma,\zeta_{1}}^{(\bar{\sigma})}
is negative, see Eq. (
366
).
Here, the energies in the arguments of the digamma functions read

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{\zeta_{1}}=\epsilon_{\bar{\sigma}}+\zeta_{1}(\epsilon_{\sigma}-\epsilon)+\delta_{\zeta_{1},+1}U+\zeta_{1}{\rm Re}\;\tilde{\Sigma}_{3\sigma,\zeta_{1}}^{(\bar{\sigma})}-{\rm i}|{\rm Im}\;\tilde{\Sigma}_{3\sigma,\zeta_{1}}^{(\bar{\sigma})}|\;.

(383)

In the degenerate case,
\epsilon_{\uparrow}=\epsilon_{\downarrow}=\epsilon_{0}
, at equilibrium,
\mu_{\alpha}=\mu

\displaystyle\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma\eta}^{(\bar{\sigma})}(\epsilon)=-\eta\frac{\Gamma}{2\pi}\Bigg[\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{2\epsilon_{0}+U-\epsilon+\tilde{\Sigma}_{3\sigma,+}^{(\bar{\sigma})}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-\psi^{*}\left(\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon-\tilde{\Sigma}_{3\sigma,-}^{(\bar{\sigma})*}-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg]-{\rm i}\frac{\Gamma}{2}\;.

(384)

At the particle-hole symmetry point
\mu-\epsilon_{0}=U/2

\displaystyle\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma\eta}^{(\bar{\sigma})}(\mu)

\displaystyle=-\eta\frac{\Gamma}{2\pi}\left[{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma_{+}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma_{-}}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\right]-{\rm i}\frac{\Gamma}{2}\;,

(385)

where we used Eq. (
377
).

Appendix O
Fermi liquid behavior within the sNCA4

Assuming a symmetric coupling,
\Gamma_{L}=\Gamma_{R}=\Gamma/2
, in the degenerate case,
\epsilon_{\sigma}=\epsilon_{0}
, Eq. (
131
) yields for the SIAM linear conductance

\displaystyle G(T)=G_{0}\frac{\pi}{2}\int d\epsilon\left(-\frac{\partial f_{+}(\epsilon)}{\partial\epsilon}\right)g(\epsilon,T)\;,

(386)

where
G_{0}=2e^{2}/h
is the unit of conductance and
g(\epsilon,T):=\Gamma[-{\rm Im}\mathcal{G}^{r}_{\sigma\sigma}(\epsilon,T)/\pi]
. Integrating by parts Eq. (
386
), using the Sommerfeld expansion for the resulting integral, and expanding
g(\mu,T)
to second order in
T
, we obtain the following low-temperature expression for the linear conductance
Smirnov and Grifoni 2013

\displaystyle G(T)\simeq G_{0}\frac{\pi}{2}\left[g(\mu,0)+\partial_{T}g(\mu,0)T+\partial_{T}^{2}g(\mu,0)T^{2}/2+\pi^{2}k_{\rm B}^{2}\partial_{\epsilon}^{2}g(\mu,0)T^{2}/6\right]\;.

(387)

From Eq. (
246
), we find for the temperature derivatives of the function
g(\epsilon,T)
, calculated at the particle-hole symmetry point,

\displaystyle\partial_{T}g(\mu,0)

\displaystyle=\frac{3U\Gamma^{2}}{2\pi}\frac{\partial_{T}{\rm Re}\tilde{\Sigma}_{-}(\mu,0)}{[D(\mu,0)]^{2}}\;,

(388)

\displaystyle\partial^{2}_{T}g(\mu,0)

\displaystyle=\frac{3U\Gamma^{2}}{2\pi}\frac{[D(\mu,0)]^{2}\partial^{2}_{T}{\rm Re}\tilde{\Sigma}_{-}(\mu,0)+2UD(\mu,0)[\partial_{T}{\rm Re}\tilde{\Sigma}_{-}(\mu,0)]^{2}}{[D(\mu,0)]^{4}}\;,

where
D(\epsilon,T);=U^{2}/4+3\Gamma^{2}/4-U{\rm Re}\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma,-}(\epsilon,T)
.
The relation
\psi(1/2+z)=2\psi(2z)-\psi(z)-2\ln(2)
and the asymptotic expansion of the digamma function
\psi(z)\sim\ln(z)+1/2z-1/12z^{2}

Wolfram_digamma
, give, in the low-temperature limit, with
Z=X+{\rm i}Y
, the approximation

\displaystyle\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{Z}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\sim\ln\left(\frac{|Z|}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)+{\rm i}\arctan\left(\frac{Y}{X}\right)+\frac{1}{24}\left(\frac{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}{Z}\right)^{2}\;.

(389)

Allowing for a temperature-dependent argument
Z=Z(T)
and using the above expression we obtain

\displaystyle\partial_{T}\;\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{Z(T)}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)

\displaystyle\sim-\frac{1}{T}+\frac{(\pi k_{\rm B})^{2}}{3[Z(T)]^{2}}T+\left[\frac{1}{Z(T)}-\frac{(\pi k_{\rm B}T)^{2}}{3[Z(T)]^{3}}\right]\partial_{T}\;Z(T)\;,

(390)

\displaystyle\partial_{T}^{2}\;\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{Z(T)}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)

\displaystyle\sim\frac{1}{T^{2}}+\frac{(\pi k_{\rm B})^{2}}{3[Z(T)]^{2}}+T\partial_{T}\frac{(\pi k_{\rm B})^{2}}{3[Z(T)]^{2}}+\partial_{T}\left[\frac{1}{Z(T)}-\frac{(\pi k_{\rm B}T)^{2}}{3[Z(T)]^{3}}\right]\partial_{T}\;Z(T)+\left[\frac{1}{Z(T)}-\frac{(\pi k_{\rm B}T)^{2}}{3[Z(T)]^{3}}\right]\partial_{T}^{2}\;Z(T)\;.

These results can be applied to the self-energy
\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma,-}(\mu)=\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma,-}^{(\sigma)}(\mu)+\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma,-}^{(\bar{\sigma})}(\mu)
at the particle-hole symmetry point

\displaystyle\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma,-}(\mu,T)=\frac{3\Gamma}{4\pi}\Bigg\{{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma_{+}(T)}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)-{\rm Re}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma_{-}(T)}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg\}-{\rm i}\frac{\Gamma}{2}\;,

(391)

with

\Gamma_{\pm}(T)=\Gamma\Bigg[1\pm\frac{2}{\pi}{\rm Im}\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\Gamma/2}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}+{\rm i}\frac{U/2}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg]\;.

Equation (
389
) also yields

\displaystyle\partial_{T}\Gamma_{\pm}(T)=\mp\frac{16\pi k_{\rm B}^{2}}{3}\frac{\Gamma^{2}U}{(\Gamma^{2}+U^{2})^{2}}T\;.

(392)

As a result,

\displaystyle\partial_{T}\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma,-}(\mu,0)

\displaystyle=0\;,

(393)

\displaystyle{\rm and}\qquad\partial_{T}^{2}\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma,-}(\mu,0)

\displaystyle=\frac{\Gamma\pi k_{\rm B}^{2}}{4}\Bigg\{\left(\frac{1}{[\Gamma_{+}(0)]^{2}}-\frac{1}{[\Gamma_{-}(0)]^{2}}\right)-\left(\frac{1}{\Gamma_{+}(0)}+\frac{1}{\Gamma_{-}(0)}\right)\frac{16\Gamma^{2}U}{\pi(\Gamma^{2}+U^{2})^{2}}\Bigg\}\;.

From
Eq. (
388
)

\displaystyle\partial_{T}g(\mu,0)

\displaystyle=0\;,\qquad\partial_{T}^{2}g(\mu,0)=\frac{3U\Gamma^{2}}{2\pi}\frac{\partial^{2}_{T}{\rm Re}\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma,-}(\mu,0)}{[U^{2}/4+3\Gamma^{2}/4-U{\rm Re}\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma,-}(\mu,0)]^{2}}\neq 0\;.

(394)

The general expression for the NCA4 Green’s function at equilibrium, conveniently rewritten as

\mathcal{G}^{R}_{\sigma\sigma}(\epsilon)=\frac{\epsilon-\epsilon_{\sigma}-U+{\rm i}3\Gamma/2+U\langle n_{\bar{\sigma}}\rangle}{(\epsilon-\epsilon_{\sigma}+{\rm i}\Gamma/2)(\epsilon-\epsilon_{\sigma}-U+{\rm i}3\Gamma/2)+U\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma,-}(\epsilon)}\;,

(395)

yields for the derivative with respect to
\epsilon
of the function
g(\epsilon,T)
calculated at the particle-hole symmetry point and in the degenerate case

\displaystyle\partial^{2}_{\epsilon}g(\mu,0)=

\displaystyle\frac{\Gamma/\pi}{[D(\mu,0)]^{2}}\left[7\Gamma+2U\partial_{\epsilon}{\rm Re}\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma,-}(\mu,0)+(3/2)\Gamma U\partial^{2}_{\epsilon}{\rm Re}\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma,-}(\mu,0)\right]

(396)

\displaystyle+\frac{\Gamma/\pi}{[D(\mu,0)]^{3}}\left\{12\Gamma^{3}-3\Gamma U^{2}\left[\partial_{\epsilon}{\rm Re}\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma,-}(\mu,0)\right]^{2}\right\}\;.

For an energy-independent argument
Z
we have for the derivatives with respect to the energy
Lahiri et al. 2020

\displaystyle\partial_{\epsilon}\;\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{Z}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg|_{\epsilon=\mu,\;T=0}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sin(\varphi)+{\rm i}\cos(\varphi)}{|Z|}\;,

(397)

\displaystyle\ \partial_{\epsilon}^{2}\;\psi\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{Z}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}+{\rm i}\frac{\epsilon-\mu}{2\pi k_{\rm B}T}\right)\Bigg|_{\epsilon=\mu,\;T=0}

\displaystyle=\frac{\cos(2\varphi)-{\rm i}\sin(2\varphi)}{|Z|^{2}}\;,

where
Z=|Z|\exp({\rm i}\varphi)
. Using Eqs. (
374
) and (
384
), this entails

\displaystyle\partial_{\epsilon}{\rm Re}\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma,-}(\mu,0)=

\displaystyle-\frac{\Gamma}{2\pi}\frac{\sin(\varphi_{+})}{|Z_{+}|}\;,

(398)

\displaystyle\partial^{2}_{\epsilon}{\rm Re}\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma,-}(\mu,0)=

\displaystyle\frac{\Gamma}{2\pi}\left[\frac{\cos(2\varphi_{+})}{|Z_{+}|}-\frac{1}{\Gamma_{-}}\right]+\frac{\Gamma}{4\pi}\left[\frac{1}{\Gamma_{+}^{2}}-\frac{1}{\Gamma_{-}^{2}}\right]\;,

where
\varphi_{+}=\arctan(2\mu/\Gamma_{+})
and
|Z_{+}|=\sqrt{(2\mu)^{2}+\Gamma_{+}^{2}}
.
The vanishing linear term in the low-temperature expansion of the linear conductance, Eq. (
387
), and the resulting quadratic dependence on
T
indicate that the sNCA4 displays at low temperature a Fermi liquid behavior. Nevertheless, the saturation value at
T=0
differs in the sNCA4 from the correct value
g(\mu,0)=2/\pi
.
